It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck is a well-built conference talk. Each slide carries one idea under a headline title with a short subtitle, and the argument builds in order: S Curves (3-7), the two then three conversations (8-9), then ecommerce, TV and the 'Is that a tech company?' refrain, then the next S Curves and regulation. The charts are simple and legible, with one red series or a highlighted bar against grey (14, 16, 44, 46), and the S Curve build across 3-6 makes an abstract idea easy to follow. Weaknesses are small type for subtitles, sources and legends at room distance, plain divider and quote slides (13, 30, 34), and a few crowded visuals such as the logo wall on 18 and the stacked bars on 33. I saw only 72 of 126 pages, so the later regulation section is unchecked.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the S Curve build on slides 3-6 and the recurring circle diagrams, which carry the structure in the spoken talk, along with the red-versus-grey emphasis in the charts.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge the subtitles, chart legends and source lines, and replace the illegible logo wall on slide 18 with a few named examples.

**What would change the score:** I would add direct labels and callouts on the key data points, for example the crossover on slide 38, so the headline claim is visible in the chart itself.

_Confidence medium. 72 of 126 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and then frames the talk around S Curves, showing how technologies move from stupid to exciting to boring and asking what comes after smartphones. It moves into what happens when everyone is online, using ecommerce, TV and platform examples with simple bar and line charts, then covers the next S Curves, and closes with regulation and policy, including anti-trust, digital extraterritoriality and the idea that tech is becoming a regulated industry. The structure is a sequence of section dividers, one-idea slides with headline titles and short subtitles, and recurring diagrams such as the circles and S Curve. Slides worth studying include the S Curve sequence on slides 3 to 6, which builds one diagram across several steps, and the market share breakdowns on slides 79 to 81, where a stacked bar chart carries the argument. The deck ends with a thank-you slide that repeats the title details.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about US ecommerce sales?**

Slide 14 states that US ecommerce is now over $600bn, shown in 2019 dollars. Slide 15 adds that ecommerce is still 'only' 16% of addressable retail.

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about S Curves?**

Slide 3 says new technology generally goes from stupid to exciting to boring. Slide 6 asks what the next S Curve will be after smartphones.

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about Amazon's platforms?**

Slide 23 states that platforms for others are now a third of Amazon revenue. Slide 24 adds that 60% of sales on Amazon are through the third party marketplace.

**How is the Benedict Evans deck structured?**

Slide 9 lists three conversations: what happens when everyone is online, what the next S Curves are, and regulation and policy. Section divider slides such as slides 10, 35 and 51 introduce each topic.

## How to read the slide sections

Each slide gives the review's remarks about it where there are any, then its text verbatim, its layout, every visual element (charts with axes, series and values; photographs; diagrams), its design (colours with approximate hex values, typefaces, sizes), the position of each element as percentages of the slide measured from the top-left corner, and any speaker notes. Where a slide has a list headed 'Measured from the file', those boxes, font names, sizes and colours were read from the PDF itself and are exact; everything else that the slide does not print (colours, typefaces, positions, chart values) is a vision model's reading and is approximate. **Layout**

Full-width header band across the top with the title left-aligned at about x 20% and a subtitle beneath it; below sits a white body band containing three vertical white columns separated by thin light-grey gutters, labelled Stupid, Exciting! and Boring, with a red S-curve rising from bottom-left to the right. A grey footer band holds the author line and page number at the lower right, with the page number at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

A single curved line chart-like diagram with no axes. From the right edge of the circle a red line continues with a filled red triangular arrowhead pointing right, ending just before the right gutter at about x 75%, y 40%. The curve enters the circle at its left side. No photographs, logos or icons. Columns are plain white rectangles with light grey (#F1F1F1) vertical separators about 0.4% of slide width.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the S Curve build on slides 3-6 and the recurring circle diagrams, which carry the structure in the spoken talk, along with the red-versus-grey emphasis in the charts.

**Text**

At this stage, we ask how we can use it…
What becomes possible now that smartphones are mature and widely deployed?
Stupid
Exciting!
Boring
Smart
Phones
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
5

**Layout**

Two header lines sit left-aligned at the top, starting at about 20% of slide width, with the title above the subtitle. Below them a white band runs full width from about 20.5% to 86% of slide height, divided into vertical columns by thin light-grey gutters: Stupid, Exciting! and Boring column headings are centred in the first three columns, and the Boring column is followed by an empty column to the right. A grey footer band holds the credit line bottom-right with the slide number at the far right.

**Visuals**

One diagram: a single grey S-curve (stroke about #7F7F7F, roughly 0.3% of slide height wide) starts at the bottom-left of the white band at about x 20%, y 85%, runs flat then rises steeply through the Exciting column and levels off into the Boring column, ending at a grey filled circle labelled Smart Phones. From the circle's right edge a short solid grey arrow (#7F7F7F) points right to an arrowhead at about x 75.5%, y 40.5%. Three red dotted curved arrows (about #E8393A) fan upward from the right side of the circle, each ending in a small upward-pointing arrowhead at about y 33%, x 60%, 63% and 68%. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from about y 20.5% to 86.1%. Flat minimal layout. Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands; central band white #FFFFFF. Title and column headings in a high-contrast Roman serif (Photina-like) in dark grey #545454, regular weight; subtitle in the same serif at smaller size. Footer text in Helvetica Neue sans-serif, grey #A6A6A6, the name in bold. Title about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, column headings about 3.0%, footer about 1.9%. Shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners; the circle is a solid #7F7F7F disc; the curve and arrow are #7F7F7F strokes; red dotted arrows are thin dotted strokes.

### Slide 6

What is the next generational change?
Stupid
Exciting!
Boring
Smart
Phones
?
Benedict Evans — February 2020
6

**Layout**

A full-width grey header band holds the left-aligned title and subtitle, starting at x 20.1%, with the title near the top and the subtitle beneath it. Below that, a white band runs full-bleed from the right edge across the slide, divided into vertical columns by thin grey gutters, with the labels Stupid, Exciting! and Boring centred in the first three columns. A grey footer band holds the right-aligned credit line and the page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

A grey S-curve line (#7F7F7F, about 0.4% of slide height thick) starts at the bottom-left edge of the white band near x 20%, y 85%, flattens, then rises steeply through the Exciting! column and ends at a grey filled circle labelled Smart Phones in the Boring column. From the circle's right edge a short solid grey line with a filled grey triangular arrowhead points right toward the column gutter. Three red dotted curved arrows (#EB483F) fan up and to the right from the circle's right side, each ending in an upward arrowhead, and stop above the Boring column. In the lower right, a pink circle with a dotted red outline contains a bold red question mark, and a dotted red line leaves its right edge and runs off the right edge of the slide. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white band (#FFFFFF) across the middle. Light grey background #F1F1F1 with a white content band. Column labels are the same serif in #545454 at about 3.0%. Circle labels and the question mark are sans-serif (Helvetica Neue, regular and bold). Footer text is sans-serif in light grey #A6A6A6 at about 1.9%, with the name in bold. Accents are grey #7F7F7F and red #EB483F. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; the pink circle is #FBDAD9.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Four columns of arrows and labels compress fifty years of platform shifts into one clean timeline with consistent typography.

**Text**

Because that’s been the model for 50 years
The tech industry has had a new centre roughly every 15 years
Mainframes
PCs
Web
Smartphones
IBM
Microsoft / Intel
Google / FB / Amazon
Apple / Google
Big companies
All companies
Middle-class families
Everyone
Benedict Evans
–– February 2020
7

**Layout**

A full-width title block sits at the top, with the title left-aligned at about x 20% and the subtitle directly beneath it. Below, a white band spans the slide from about 20.5% to 86% of the height, divided into five vertical columns by thin grey gutters; the first four columns carry a labelled step, and the fifth is empty apart from the red arrow. A grey footer strip at the bottom holds the author, date and slide number, left-aligned in a single row.

**Visuals**

Five curved arrows, each a smooth S-shaped stroke that rises from the lower left of one column to an arrowhead at the upper right of the next. Arrows 1 to 4 are solid mid-grey (#808080), about 0.4% of slide height in stroke weight, each ending in a solid grey triangular arrowhead. Arrow 5 is the final rightmost arrow, in red (#E8413C), drawn as a dotted stroke with a solid red triangular arrowhead, starting at the left edge of the fifth column and rising to the upper right. No photographs, logos, charts or icons. Each arrow starts where the previous one ends, forming a continuous stepped rise across the columns.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from about 20.5% to 86% of height. Flat, minimal look with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Background is light grey #F1F1F1, with a white #FFFFFF band behind the columns. The title and subtitle are dark grey #545454 in a regular-weight serif (Photina MT). Column labels and body text are dark grey #545454 in a regular sans-serif (Helvetica Neue). Footer text is light grey #A6A6A6, with the author name in bold. The title is about 5.6% of slide height, the subtitle and column labels about 2.6% and 3.0%, and body and footer text about 1.85%. Lines are grey #808080 curves; the single accent is red #E8413C.

### Slide 8

**Text**

So, two conversations today / What now and what next? / What happens / when everyone is / online? / What are the next / S Curves? / Benedict Evans / –– February 2020 / 8

**Layout**

Single slide with a left-aligned title block at the top left, starting at about 20% from the left edge and 5.5% from the top. Two equal white circles sit side by side in the middle band, left-aligned to the title margin, with a third white circle at the right under a faint pale panel that spans the right side from about 30% to 84% of the height. The footer runs along the bottom right, with the author name and date at about 75% to 91% across and the slide number at about 95%.

**Visuals**

Two white circles with outlines, each about 22.7% of slide width and 40.4% of slide height. The left circle has a mid-grey outline (about #808080) and the right circle has a dark slate outline (about #2F4A50), both about 0.3% of slide height in stroke width. Each circle holds a centred question in serif type. A third white circle (x 73.6%, y 33.3%) is positioned behind a pale #F2F2F2 rectangle at x 72.0%, y 30.0%, w 25.9%, h 54.1%, so it is not visible in the render. No photographs, charts, icons or logos.

**Design**

Background is a flat light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. The title is a serif (Photina-style, regular weight) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. The subtitle is the same serif in #545454 at about 2.6% of slide height. Circle labels are the same serif in #545454 at about 3.3% of slide height. The footer is a sans-serif (Helvetica Neue) in light grey #A6A6A6 at about 1.9% of slide height, with 'Benedict Evans' in bold. Circles are plain white fills with thin outlines and no shadow. The pale panel is a flat #F2F2F2 rectangle with no border.

### Slide 9

**Text**

So, two three conversations today
Connecting the world has had consequences far outside tech
What happens
when everyone is
online?
What are the next
S Curves?
Regulation and
policy
Benedict Evans — February 2020
9

**Layout**

Three equal white circles sit in a horizontal row across the middle of the slide, their centres at roughly 29%, 57% and 85% of the slide width and about 53% of the slide height. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.1% near the top, with the title block starting at y 5.5%. The footer sits bottom-right at y 91.1%: author name, em dash and date, then the page number at x 95.0%. There is no visible grid or column rule.

**Visuals**

Three empty circles (shapes, no photographs or charts). Circle 1, left: white fill #FFFFFF, mid-grey outline #808080 about 0.3% of slide height thick, containing the text 'What happens / when everyone is / online?'. Circle 2, centre: white fill #FFFFFF, dark slate outline #37474F about 0.3% thick, containing 'What are the next / S Curves?'. Circle 3, right: white fill #FFFFFF, red outline #E74C3C about 0.3% thick, containing 'Regulation and / policy'. Each circle is 22.7% wide by 40.4% high, so they are ellipses sized to a circle on the slide. No shadows, gradients or icons. The word 'two' in the title is struck through with a single horizontal line in #545454.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat light-grey background. Titles and body text in dark grey #545454 in a high-contrast serif (Photina, regular weight). Footer in light grey #A6A6A6, sans-serif (Helvetica Neue). Title about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, circle labels about 3.3%, footer about 1.9%. Circle outlines are thin strokes with no corner radius because they are circles. Accent colour is the red outline of the third circle. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 10

**Text**

What happens when / everyone is online?
Benedict Evans — February 2020
10

**Layout**

Single title slide with a full-slide dark teal background and a lighter translucent rectangular panel inset from the left and right (x 17.3% to 95.4%, y 20.4% to 81.5%). The title sits in the upper-left of the panel, left-aligned at x 20.2%, over two lines. A thin white horizontal rule runs along the bottom edge of the panel. The footer line (author, date, page number) sits bottom-right at y 91.1%, with the page number at the far right margin (ends at x 95.6%).

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid dark slate-teal #36494E. Flat dark slate-teal background #36494E with a slightly lighter panel (white at 4% opacity over the background, roughly #3D4F54). Title text is white #FFFFFF in a high-contrast serif (PhotinaMT, roman, regular weight), about 9.3% of slide height per line with generous line spacing. Footer text is grey #A6A6A6 in a neo-grotesque sans-serif (Helvetica Neue): 'Benedict Evans' bold, the rest regular, about 1.85% of slide height. Panel has square corners, no shadow, no gradient. A white horizontal rule about 0.2% of slide height thick runs along the panel bottom edge, from x 17.3% to 95.4%, at about y 81.5%. Title is the dominant element; no body text.

### Slide 11

**Text**

Standing on the shoulders of giants
New possibilities at new scale
Density &
penetration
Consumer
expectation
Platforms
Benedict Evans — February 2020
11

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block starts at about 20% from the left edge with the title near the top and the subtitle directly beneath it. Three equal red circles sit in a single horizontal row across the centre-lower area, joined by two pale arrows between them, spanning roughly 19% to 96% of the width. The footer sits bottom right with the author name and date followed by the slide number at the far right of the bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Three filled red circles, each about 22% of slide width and 39% of slide height, with centred white serif labels: 'Density & penetration', 'Consumer expectation', 'Platforms'. Between circles one and two and between two and three sits a pale right-pointing block arrow in #F2F2F2 with a very low-contrast appearance against the background, about 4% wide and 10.4% tall, with a flat shaft and triangular head. No photographs, charts, or logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1. Flat light grey background #F1F1F1 with no texture. Title and subtitle in grey #545454 in a serif typeface resembling Photina (a transitional serif, regular weight); title is large, subtitle small. Body labels in white #FFFFFF serif, regular weight. Circles are solid #EB483F with no outline and no shadow. Arrows are #F2F2F2 flat shapes with no outline, almost invisible against the background. Footer uses Helvetica Neue, the author name in bold and the rest in regular, all in grey #A6A6A6. Title size about 5.6% of slide height, body labels about 3.3%, subtitle about 2.6%, footer about 1.9%. No gradients, corner radii or shadows.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A single bar chart with a direct-labelled headline figure makes the scale of ecommerce immediately readable.

**Text**

Front of mind: ecommerce is big…
US ecommerce is now over $600bn
US ecommerce sales ($bn, 2019 dollars)
700
600
500
400
300
200
100
0
2000
2005
2010
2015
2019
Source: US Census
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
14

**Layout**

Full-width header band (top 20.5% of slide) and footer band (bottom 13.9%) in light grey, with a white content band between them. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.1% in the header, starting on the same left edge as the source line in the footer. A single chart spans x 18% to 94.5% of the content band, with the chart title left-aligned above it at x 20.5%. Footer holds the source line at left, and author, date and page number right-aligned to x 95.6%. Page number '14' sits at the bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

Chart: line chart with diamond markers, titled 'US ecommerce sales ($bn, 2019 dollars)'. Y-axis: $bn, 0 to 700, gridlines every 100, labels 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700. X-axis: years 2000 to 2019, labels shown at 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015, 2019. Single series in red line with red diamond markers; no legend; no values printed. Values about: 2000 about 42; 2001 about 50; 2002 about 64; 2003 about 80; 2004 about 99; 2005 about 120; 2006 about 144; 2007 about 169; 2008 about 167; 2009 about 174; 2010 about 200; 2011 about 228; 2012 about 259; 2013 about 290; 2014 about 327; 2015 about 374; 2016 about 425; 2017 about 482; 2018 about 534; 2019 about 616. Line stroke red approx #EE4036, about 3 px wide. Gridlines dotted light grey. X-axis baseline solid thin grey. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands with a white #FFFFFF central band. Title in a serif display face (Photina MT, regular weight, roman) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height, with the subtitle in the same family at about 2.6%. Chart title and axis labels in Helvetica Neue (bold for chart title, regular for labels) in grey #545554, about 1.85% to 2.2% of slide height. Footer text in Helvetica Neue regular grey #545454 for the source line, and light grey #A6A6A6 for the author name (bold), date and page number. Accent colour is red #EE4036 for the data line and markers. Lines are flat with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

0

2019

Benedict Evans
–– February 2020
15

**Layout**

Full-width slide with a light grey #F1F1F1 header band (top about 20% of height) and a light grey footer band (below about 86%) framing a white content band between them. The title sits left-aligned at about x 20.1% in the header, with a subtitle beneath it. The chart occupies the centre-right of the white band, starting at about x 20.5% and running to about x 95.6%, with a legend at the far left (x 4.8%) in the same band. The source lines sit bottom-left at x 20.1%, and the author, date and slide number sit bottom-right in the footer band, with the page number at about x 94.4%. Left margin for text is about 20% of width; right margin is about 4%.

**Visuals**

Chart: line chart with markers, rendered as a single image (1441 by 478 px) at x 20.5%, y 36.0%, w 75.1%, h 44.3%. Chart title printed above the plot: US retail sales ($tr, 2019 dollars). Y axis: linear, labelled 0 to 4 in $tr (units from title), gridlines dotted light grey at 1, 2, 3 and 4, with a solid thin grey baseline at 0. X axis: years 2000 to 2019, one point per year, with labels printed at 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015 and 2019. Series 1, Addressable retail*, red line (#E8453C) with red diamond markers; values not printed, so approximate: 2000 about 2.9, 2001 about 2.9, 2002 about 2.95, 2003 about 3.0, 2004 about 3.1, 2005 about 3.2, 2006 about 3.2, 2007 about 3.25, 2008 about 3.1, 2009 about 3.05, 2010 about 3.1, 2011 about 3.15, 2012 about 3.2, 2013 about 3.25, 2014 about 3.3, 2015 about 3.45, 2016 about 3.5, 2017 about 3.6, 2018 about 3.65, 2019 about 3.8. Series 2, Ecommerce, grey line (#A6A6A6) with grey diamond markers; values approximate: 2000 about 0.05, rising gradually to 2010 about 0.2, 2015 about 0.38, 2019 about 0.6. Legend: top-left of chart area at x 4.8%, y 35.5% to 39.5%, showing a red diamond with Addressable retail* and a grey diamond with Ecommerce, stacked vertically. No highlighted points; no annotations.

**Design**

Background: Solid #F1F1F1 light grey band across the top and bottom, with a #FFFFFF white band across the middle. Two-tone grey bands with a white centre: header and footer #F1F1F1, content band #FFFFFF, no borders or shadows. Title text colour #545454, subtitle #545454, chart title #545454 bold, axis labels #545454, legend text #535353, footer author and page number #A6A6A6, source lines #545454. Accent colours: red #E8453C for the addressable retail series, grey #A6A6A6 for the ecommerce series. Title typeface is PhotinaMT, a serif with regular weight; subtitle also PhotinaMT regular. Chart and body text is HelveticaNeue (sans-serif), regular weight, with HelveticaNeue-Bold for the chart title and author name. Title size about 5.6% of slide height (cap height roughly 3.9%); subtitle about 2.6%; body and chart labels about 1.8% to 1.9%; source lines about 1.8%. Lines: chart series about 3 px stroke, gridlines hairline dotted grey.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 16

**Text**

Globally, the USA is in the middle of the pack
China leapfrogged physical retail, while the UK and SK are also far ahead of the USA
Ecommerce share of retail, 2019
30%
25%
20%
15%
10%
5%
0%
China
UK
SK
USA
France
Japan
Germany
Russia
Brazil
India
Source: McKinsey, BLS
Benedict Evans
–– February 2020
16

**Layout**

Two horizontal bands frame the slide: a light grey header band across the top (about 0 to 20.5% of height) holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at about x 20.1%, and a light grey footer band from about 86.1% to the bottom. A white content band sits between them, holding a single full-width bar chart whose plot area runs from about x 20.6% to 95.3%. The source line is left-aligned at x 20.1% in the footer; the author, date and slide number are right-side footer items, with the page number at about x 94.4%.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, vertical columns. Title: 'Ecommerce share of retail, 2019' (bold, left-aligned above the plot). Value axis: 0% to 30%, labelled every 5% (0%, 5%, 10%, 15%, 20%, 25%, 30%), dotted light-grey gridlines at each labelled value. Category axis: countries, no axis title. No data labels are printed on the bars; values below are estimates marked 'about' from bar heights. Series, left to right: China about 25%; UK about 22%; SK about 21.5%; USA about 14%; France about 10%; Japan about 9%; Germany about 9%; Russia about 5%; Brazil about 4%; India about 3%. No legend (single series). Highlighted point: the USA bar is red #EC473F while all other bars are mid grey #7F7F7F. Bars are solid rectangles of equal width (about 3% of slide width), with gaps of about 4.5% between them, sitting on a thin grey baseline at 0%.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from about y 20.5% to 86.1%. Restrained, minimal editorial look. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 with a white content area between them. Title in a high-contrast serif (Photina-style, regular weight) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, about 2.6% of slide height, dark grey #545454. Chart title in a bold neo-grotesque sans (Helvetica Neue Bold) about 2.2% of slide height, #545554. Axis and category labels in Helvetica Neue regular about 1.85% of slide height, #535353. Footer source line in Helvetica Neue regular #545454; author name in Helvetica Neue Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in Helvetica Neue regular #A6A6A6, all about 1.85% of slide height. Accent colour: red #EC473F used only on the USA bar. Bars grey #7F7F7F. Gridlines thin dotted light grey, baseline solid thin grey. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or icons.

Expanding what ecommerce means
Ecommerce has evolved past commodity products in brown cardboard boxes
Books, commodities

17

**Layout**

Full-width three-band layout on a light grey field. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at about x 20% in a grey header band across the top, with the title near the top margin. Below, three white full-width horizontal bands are separated by thin grey gaps, each holding a left-aligned label at x 20%, a red dotted arrow at centre-right, and a left-aligned label at x 73%. A grey footer strip carries the right-aligned footer line 'Benedict Evans — February 2020' and the page number 17 at the far right, near the bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Three horizontal dotted red arrows, one in each white band. Each is a straight dotted line with a solid filled triangular arrowhead at its right end, about 49% to 65% of slide width, drawn in a red roughly #E8473F with small round dots. Arrow 1 sits at about 30% of slide height in the first band, arrow 2 at about 53% in the second band, and arrow 3 at about 76% in the third band. No photographs, logos, charts or icons.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1. White bands #FFFFFF with thin #F2F2F2 gaps between them. Titles and body text in dark grey #545454 in a transitional serif (PhotinaMT-like, close to Photina/Century style), regular weight. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle and row labels about 2.6% and 3.3% respectively. Footer in a sans-serif (Helvetica Neue), with 'Benedict Evans' in bold and the rest regular, about 1.9% of slide height, colour #A6A6A6. Accents are red arrows roughly #E8473F. Lines are dotted with solid triangular heads; no shadows, no corner radii, no gradients.

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Enlarge the subtitles, chart legends and source lines, and replace the illegible logo wall on slide 18 with a few named examples.

**Text**

Powering the ‘D2C’ explosion…
Several hundred (at least) new online-only or online-first brands
Benedict Evans
–– February 2020
18

**Layout**

Left-aligned title and subtitle sit in the upper-left at x 20.1%, with the title's top at 5.5% of height and the subtitle beneath it. A single bordered panel of three equal-ish columns (Apparel & Fashion, Baby Care/Beauty/Beer-Wine/Education/Food stack, Hobbies/Home/Personal Care/Wellness stack) fills the centre-left from 20.1% to 85.2% horizontally and 20.9% to 86.1% vertically. The footer 'Benedict Evans –– February 2020' is right-aligned near the bottom at y 91.1%, with the page number 18 at the far right (x 94.4%). Margins are roughly 20% left and 15% right, and the content is not on a strict grid beyond the three columns.

**Visuals**

One large logo-grid graphic (the IAB '250 Direct Brands to Watch' chart) occupying the whole panel. Top-left cell is a light grey (#DCDCDC) box holding the IAB logo: a dark grey/black and red triangular arrow mark with 'iab.' in white, a large white '250' and red 'BRANDS' with black 'DIRECT' and 'TO WATCH'. Below it, the three columns are organised under red category bars (#E63329) with white bold capital labels and a white pictogram icon at left of each label. **Design**

Background: Flat light grey #F1F1F1 solid colour. Light grey slide background #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Title in a serif (PhotinaMT, close to Photina/Century-style) regular weight, colour #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif regular, #545454, about 2.6% of height. Footer and page number in Helvetica Neue, about 1.9% of height, colour #A6A6A6; 'Benedict Evans' in bold, the date and page number regular. Panel: white (#FFFFFF) cells with thin light grey dividers, category bars in red #E63329 with white bold sans-serif capitals (about 1.2% of height). The IAB header cell is #DCDCDC. Logos are small, mostly black, grey or brand-coloured wordmarks. No shadows, rounded corners or gradients are visible; the panel is a flat rectangle with square corners.

**Layout**

Two-band frame: a light grey header band across the top about 20% of the height holds the left-aligned serif title and subtitle at x 20.1%, and a grey footer band from about 86% to the bottom holds the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. Between them a white band carries a single bar chart with its heading at x 20.5%, the y-axis labels at x about 18.6% and the plot running to about x 91%. Left margin of the text is about 20%, right margin of the page number about 5.6%.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'Shopify GMV ($bn)'. Vertical axis labelled in $bn with gridlines at 0, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50, 60 and 70 (range 0 to 70), drawn as light grey dotted horizontal lines with a solid thin grey baseline at 0. Horizontal axis shows years 2014, 2015, 2016, 2017, 2018 and 2019 with no axis title. One series of six red bars, no data labels printed; values are read against the gridlines: 2014 about 3.6, 2015 about 7.4, 2016 about 15.1, 2017 about 26.1, 2018 about 40.8, 2019 about 60.9. No legend and no highlighted points; all bars share the same colour #EB483F.

Axis labels and source in Helvetica Neue regular #535353 at about 1.85%. Author name bold sans #A6A6A6 and date and page number regular sans #A6A6A6 at about 1.85%. Bars solid red #EB483F with square corners and no shadow. Gridlines thin dotted grey. No gradients, no shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 21

**Text**

Meanwhile: Amazon! / Amazon’s revenue continues to grow at 20% a year / Amazon annual revenue ($bn) / 300 / 250 / 200 / 150 / 100 / 50 / 0 / 1995 / 2000 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 2019 / Source: Amazon / Benedict Evans — February 2020 / 21

**Layout**

Two-band layout: a light grey header band across the top fifth holds the title and subtitle, left-aligned at x 20.1% with the text block starting at the same margin. A white central band carries a single line chart spanning about x 20.5% to 95.6%, with y-axis labels to its left and year labels beneath. A grey footer band holds the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the presenter credit, date and slide number at right, with the slide number ending near x 95.6%.

**Visuals**

Chart: line chart with diamond markers, titled 'Amazon annual revenue ($bn)' in bold sans-serif above the plot. Y-axis: revenue in $bn, range 0 to 300, labelled ticks at 0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled tick. X-axis: calendar years 1995 to 2019, one point per year (25 points), labelled ticks at 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015 and 2019; a thin solid grey baseline sits at 0. No legend; single series. No values are printed on the points, so values are estimates marked about: 1995 about 0; 1996 about 0.5; 1997 about 1; 1998 about 1.5; 1999 about 2; 2000 about 3; 2001 about 3.5; 2002 about 4.5; 2003 about 5.5; 2004 about 7; 2005 about 8.5; 2006 about 11; 2007 about 15; 2008 about 19; 2009 about 25; 2010 about 34; 2011 about 48; 2012 about 61; 2013 about 74; 2014 about 89; 2015 about 107; 2016 about 136; 2017 about 178; 2018 about 233; 2019 about 280. Line is red (#E8453C) about 3 px thick with red diamond markers. No highlighted points beyond the endpoint trend.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide, with a white (#FFFFFF) band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Flat, minimal look with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Title in a light-weight serif (Photina, regular) in grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height for the title cap-height block; subtitle in the same serif at about 2.6%. Chart title and axis labels in Helvetica Neue (sans-serif); chart title bold #545554 at about 2.2%, tick labels regular #545554 at about 1.85%. Source line regular Helvetica Neue #545454 at about 1.85%. Footer presenter name bold Helvetica Neue #A6A6A6 at about 1.85%, with date and slide number regular #A6A6A6. Accent colour is the red chart line #E8453C. Gridlines are thin dotted light grey (#B0B0B0, about 0.5 px). Header and footer bands are #F1F1F1; the central band is white.

### Slide 22

**Text**

Though only half is direct ecommerce / But it has become about more than just plain ecommerce / Amazon annual revenue ($bn) / Revenue / Ecommerce / 300 / 250 / 200 / 150 / 100 / 50 / 0 / 1995 / 2000 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 2019 / Source: Amazon / Benedict Evans / –– February 2020 / 22

**Layout**

Full-width horizontal banding: a light grey header band holds the title and subtitle left-aligned at about 20% from the left edge, a white band holds the chart with its title at the same left edge, and a light grey footer band holds the source line at left and the author, date and slide number at right. The chart occupies roughly 20% to 96% of the width, and the legend sits in a left margin column at about 5% from the left edge. The slide number 22 sits at the bottom right, about 94% across, in the footer.

**Visuals**

One line chart, titled 'Amazon annual revenue ($bn)'. Type: line chart with diamond markers. Y axis: 0 to 300 in steps of 50, unit $bn, labels at left, dotted light grey horizontal gridlines at each step. X axis: years 1995 to 2019, labels at 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015 and 2019, solid thin grey baseline at 0. Series 1 'Revenue' (red, one point per year 1995 to 2019): about 0 in 1995, about 0 in 1996, about 1 in 1997, about 1 in 1998, about 2 in 1999, about 3 in 2000, about 3 in 2001, about 4 in 2002, about 5 in 2003, about 7 in 2004, about 8 in 2005, about 11 in 2006, about 15 in 2007, about 19 in 2008, about 25 in 2009, about 34 in 2010, about 48 in 2011, about 61 in 2012, about 75 in 2013, about 89 in 2014, about 107 in 2015, about 136 in 2016, about 178 in 2017, about 233 in 2018, about 281 in 2019. Series 2 'Ecommerce' (grey, one point per year 2014 to 2019): about 69 in 2014, about 77 in 2015, about 92 in 2016, about 108 in 2017, about 123 in 2018, about 141 in 2019. No values are printed on the slide; all values are estimates. Legend: top-left of the chart, red diamond marker labelled Revenue above grey diamond marker labelled Ecommerce. Highlighted points: none; the last point of each series (2019) is the highest point on its line.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 background across the full slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from 20.5% to 86.1% of the height. Light grey header and footer bands (#F1F1F1) framing a white chart band. Title in a serif face resembling PhotinaMT, regular weight, dark grey #545454, roughly 5.6% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and axis labels in a Helvetica Neue style sans-serif; the chart title is bold #545554 at about 2.2% of slide height, the axis labels and legend are regular #545554 at about 1.85%. Accent colours: Revenue line and markers red #E8453C, Ecommerce line and markers grey #808080. Lines about 2 to 3 px wide with solid strokes; gridlines dotted hairlines in light grey. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients, flat fills.

**Layout**

Two horizontal bands: a light grey header band across the top (title and subtitle) and a light grey footer band across the bottom, with a white chart area between them. The title starts at about x 20% and sits in the upper part of the header, with the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on the same left edge. The chart is a single stacked column chart with its title left-aligned above the plot at x about 20.5%, the y-axis labels at the left of the plot, and the three category labels centred under their columns. The footer holds 'Source: Amazon' left-aligned at x 20.1% and, right-aligned to the slide edge at about 95%, the author name, date and page number 23 on one line.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart, titled 'Amazon revenue, 2019 ($bn)'. Y-axis: linear, $bn, 0 to 150, labelled at 0, 25, 50, 75, 100, 125, 150, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each label. X-axis: three categories, 'First party ecommerce', 'Platform', 'Other', with a thin solid grey baseline at 0. Series and values (no values printed on the slide; estimates marked about): First party ecommerce is a single solid grey segment, about 141. Platform is a stack of Marketplace (bottom, red, about 54) and AWS (top, pale pink, about 35), total about 89. Other is a stack of Subscriptions (bottom, mid grey, about 19), Stores (middle, lighter grey, about 17) and Ads & Other (top, very light grey, about 14), total about 50. Segments are separated by thin white lines. No legend; each segment carries its name as an inside label. Highlighted points: none; the red Marketplace segment is the only saturated colour and stands out against the greys. Bars are flat, unshaded and have no outlines; they are about 10% of slide width each, with generous gaps.

**Design**

Background: Flat #F1F1F1 light grey, with a white #FFFFFF band from about 20.5% to 86% of slide height behind the chart. Flat, minimal style. Header and footer bands #F1F1F1 with the chart area white #FFFFFF. Title in a light serif (PhotinaMT-like, regular weight) #545454, about 5.6% of slide height; subtitle in the same serif #545454, about 2.6% of height. Chart title and axis numerals in Helvetica Neue, bold for the chart title (#545554) and regular for labels (#545554), about 1.9% to 2.2% of height. Segment labels Helvetica Neue regular, white #FFFFFF on the mid and dark segments and near-black #1B1B1B on the pale AWS and Ads & Other segments. Footer text Helvetica Neue, #A6A6A6 for author, date and page number, #545454 for the source line. Accent colour: red #EC473F for Marketplace. Segment fills: AWS #F8DBD8, Marketplace #EC473F, Ads & Other #DDDDDD, Stores #A7A7A7, Subscriptions #7F8080, First party ecommerce #7F8080. Gridlines are thin dotted grey. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or icons.

### Slide 24

**Text**

Amazon is a platform for others / 60% of sales on Amazon are through the third party marketplace / Amazon GMV ($bn) / Marketplace / First party ecommerce / 300 / 250 / 200 / 150 / 100 / 50 / 0 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / Source: Amazon, @BenedictEvans / Benedict Evans — February 2020 / 24

**Layout**

Three horizontal bands: a light grey header (top 0 to 20.5%) holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%, a white content band (20.5% to 86.1%) holding the chart, and a light grey footer band (86.1% to 100%) holding the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. Content sits on a left margin of about 20% and a right margin of about 5%; the legend sits at the far left (x 3 to 16%) outside the chart column.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart, titled 'Amazon GMV ($bn)' (bold sans, top left of plot). Y axis: $bn, range 0 to 300, labelled ticks at 0, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each tick. X axis: years 2014, 2015, 2016, 2017, 2018, categories with no axis title; baseline is a thin grey line at 0. Two series stacked per column: bottom series 'First party ecommerce' in grey #7F7F7F, top series 'Marketplace' in red #EB483F. Values are not printed on the slide, so estimates: First party ecommerce about 65 (2014), about 72 (2015), about 86 (2016), about 102 (2017), about 122 (2018); Marketplace about 62 (2014), about 76 (2015), about 101 (2016), about 131 (2017), about 170 (2018); totals about 127, 149, 187, 233, 292. Legend: top entry red square 'Marketplace', below it grey square 'First party ecommerce', at top left of chart. Highlighted points: none. Bars are flat, thin white separators between segments, no data labels.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide fill, with a white #FFFFFF band across the middle (y 20.5% to 86.1%). Light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands with a white #FFFFFF content band between them. Title in a light book serif (PhotinaMT-like, regular weight) in dark grey #545454, large (about 5.6% of slide height). Subtitle in the same serif, regular, dark grey #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and axis labels in Helvetica Neue sans-serif; chart title bold #545554, tick labels regular #545454, about 1.9% of slide height. Footer author name bold sans #A6A6A6, date and page number regular sans #A6A6A6, about 1.9% of slide height. Source line regular sans #545454. Accent colours: red #EB483F for Marketplace, mid grey #7F7F7F for first-party. Bars are square-cornered, no shadows, no gradients; gridlines are thin dotted grey.

**Layout**

Four equal circles sit in a single horizontal row across the middle-lower area, evenly spaced at about 20% intervals, starting at x 19.4% and ending at x 96%. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.1% near the top, with a left margin of about 20% and a right margin of about 4%. The source line sits bottom-left at x 20.1%, and the footer (author name, date, slide number) sits bottom-right, with the slide number at x 94.4%.

**Visuals**

Four solid circles in one row, each about 16.2% wide by 28.8% tall (close to circular on the 16:9 slide). Circles 1 to 3 are mid grey #7F7F7F; circle 4 on the far right is red #EB483F. Each circle carries a centred white serif label reading, from left to right: 'Amazon sells placement for $10bn', 'Booking & Expedia pay Google $10bn', 'But Macy’s & Walmart did this too', and 'Kylie Jenner’s make-up business: $1.2bn'. No chart, photograph, logo or icon.

**Design**

Background: Flat light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat, minimal, editorial look. Background is a light warm grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Title and subtitle are dark grey #545454 in a Photina-style serif, regular weight; the title is about 5.6% of slide height and the subtitle about 2.6%. Circle labels are white #FFFFFF serif, regular weight, about 3.0% of slide height, set in three or four centred lines with tight leading. Circles are flat fills with no outline, no shadow and no gradient. Source and footer text are Helvetica Neue sans-serif at about 1.9% of slide height; the footer author name is bold #A6A6A6, the rest of the footer and the page number are regular #A6A6A6, and the source line is #545454. Accent colour is the red #EB483F, used only on the final circle.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Get it wrong? Go to the mattresses
A vacuum-packed mattress was a brilliant idea until everyone else did it
Source: GoodBed.com
Benedict Evans — February 2020
29
2019: there are
175 online
mattress
companies

**Layout**

Left-aligned single-column header with title at top-left (x 20.1%) and a one-line subtitle beneath it; three circular visuals sit in a horizontal row across the vertical middle with grey arrows between them, and the right circle holds a text statement. Source line sits bottom-left and the footer (author, date, slide number) sits bottom-right, with the slide number at the far right margin.

**Visuals**

Three circular images in a row, each about 20.7% of slide width and 36.8% of slide height, with a thin grey outline. Left: a cropped, full-circle photograph of a colourful retail mattress sale flyer, with a blue starburst background, large white-and-red block letters reading 'BIGGEST MATTRESS SALE EVER' (partly cut off as 'IGEST' and 'ATTRESS'), red starbursts and price stickers ('$699', '$1199', '$649', '$1399', '$879 OFF', '$1000 OFF', '$1170 OFF'), and red banners reading 'FREE Set-Up' and 'FREE Recycling'; dominant colours blue, white, red and yellow. Middle: a photograph of a white-and-blue striped cardboard box partly open with a white pillow on a dark mattress on a black metal bed frame, lettering 'Casper' on the box, pale wooden floor, grey wall panelling; soft natural light, dominant colours white, navy, grey and light wood. Right: a plain white circle with a 2.5px-ish red (#E74C3C-like) outline containing the text statement. Between circles: two solid grey (#B3B3B3) right-pointing block arrows, about 6% of slide width each, flat with no shadow.

**Design**

Background flat light grey #F1F1F1. Title in serif (Photina-like, regular weight) dark grey #545454, large relative to slide height (about 5.6% of slide height, capitals about 3.9%). Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Body text in the circle is the same serif at 3.3% of slide height, #545454, centred. Source line and footer in Helvetica Neue sans-serif, about 1.9% of slide height: source #545454, footer in #A6A6A6 with 'Benedict Evans' in bold. Circles: photo circles have a grey (#7F7F7F-ish) thin border; the statement circle is white #FFFFFF with a red (#E74C3C) border. Grey arrows #B3B3B3. No shadows, no gradients, no corner radii (circles).

none

Welcome to retail

600

**Layout**

Two-band layout on a full-bleed light grey field: a grey header band (top about 20% of the slide) holds the title and subtitle, left-aligned at x 20.1% with a 20% left margin. A white content panel fills the middle, with a line chart spanning x 20.2% to 95.3%, its y-axis labels at the left edge of the plot, and a text legend in the left margin at x 4.8%. A grey footer band (below about 86%) holds the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the author, date and slide number at right, with the slide number at x 94.4%, flush to a right margin of about 4.5%.

**Visuals**

Line chart, titled 'US retail sales ($bn, 2019 dollars)', with three series plotted as lines with diamond markers, 1995 to 2018. Y-axis is labelled 0, 150, 300, 450, 600 ($bn, 2019 dollars); plot range 0 to about 600, with faint dotted horizontal gridlines at 150, 300, 450 and 600 and a solid thin grey baseline at 0. X-axis is labelled 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015, 2018 with a solid thin grey baseline. Series 1, Department stores (red #E8453C diamonds and line): about 150 in 1995, 150 in 1996, 151 in 1997, 149 in 1998, 150 in 1999, 143 in 2000, 132 in 2001, 126 in 2002, 119 in 2003, 113 in 2004, 108 in 2005, 101 in 2006, 94 in 2007, 84 in 2008, 76 in 2009, 77 in 2010, 75 in 2011, 70 in 2012, 67 in 2013, 65 in 2014, 63 in 2015, 58 in 2016, 53 in 2017, 49 in 2018 (all values about). Series 2, Ecommerce (light grey #A6A6A6 diamonds and line): starts 2000 at about 41, then about 49 in 2001, 63 in 2002, 79 in 2003, 97 in 2004, 120 in 2005, 143 in 2006, 166 in 2007, 165 in 2008, 171 in 2009, 198 in 2010, 227 in 2011, 258 in 2012, 289 in 2013, 326 in 2014, 370 in 2015, 424 in 2016, 483 in 2017, 534 in 2018 (all values about). Series 3, Warehouse clubs (dark grey #4D4D4D diamonds and line): about 108 in 1995, 119 in 1996, 131 in 1997, 154 in 1998, 182 in 1999, 208 in 2000, 236 in 2001, 274 in 2002, 303 in 2003, 331 in 2004, 359 in 2005, 377 in 2006, 401 in 2007, 414 in 2008, 425 in 2009, 436 in 2010, 441 in 2011, 454 in 2012, 461 in 2013, 467 in 2014, 476 in 2015, 478 in 2016, 484 in 2017, 489 in 2018 (all values about). Legend: top-to-bottom Department stores (red diamond), Ecommerce (light grey diamond), Warehouse clubs (dark grey diamond). No highlighted points; the Ecommerce and Warehouse clubs lines cross near 2018 and near 1998 and 2005 is where Ecommerce passes Department stores.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-bleed field with a white #FFFFFF content panel across the middle band. Flat, minimal look with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 and the content panel is white #FFFFFF. Title text is a serif (Photina-style Georgia-like book serif) in dark grey #545454, regular weight, at about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle is the same serif in #545454 at about 2.6% of height. Chart title and legend text are Helvetica Neue sans-serif, chart title bold, in #545554 at about 2.2% and 1.85% of height. Axis labels are Helvetica Neue regular #545554 at about 1.85%. Source line is Helvetica Neue regular #545454 at 1.85%. Footer author name is Helvetica Neue bold #A6A6A6 and the date and slide number are Helvetica Neue regular #A6A6A6 at 1.85%. Series colours: red #E8453C, light grey #A6A6A6, dark grey #4D4D4D. Gridlines are thin dotted light grey, about #BFBFBF, 0.5 px. Baselines are thin solid grey #8C8C8C. Lines are about 3 px wide with diamond markers about 10 px across.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

Single full-width white panel offset to the right on a light grey field, with the quote block top-left inside the panel at about 20% x and 29% y; the quote is left-aligned with the body lines stacked and a blank line before the attribution. none

### Slide 33

**Text**

Retail isn’t as binary as ‘online’ and ‘offline’
Online, yes, but also rent, distance to customer, service, selection, staff costs, urgency, margin, inventory, etc, etc
US retail sales by retailer category, 2018
100%
Non-addressable retail
75%
50%
Addressable retail
25%
Ecommerce
0%
'Online versus offline'
'Retail'
Source: US Census, @BenedictEvans
*Addressable retail = ex. cars, car parts, gasoline, restaurants & bars.
Benedict Evans —– February 2020
33

**Layout**

Full-width grey header band across the top (0 to about 20.5% of height) holds the title and subtitle, left-aligned at about x 20.1%. A white content area fills the middle with a bold chart title at x 20.5%, a y-axis at the left and two stacked columns on a horizontal baseline. A grey footer band (about 90 to 100%) holds the source lines at left and the author, date and slide number at right, with the page number at x 94.4%. Margins are about 20% on the left and about 5.6% on the right.

**Visuals**

Chart: 100% stacked column chart titled 'US retail sales by retailer category, 2018'. Y-axis runs 0% to 100% in 25% steps with faint dotted horizontal gridlines at 25, 50, 75 and 100%. X-axis categories are 'Online versus offline' (left column) and 'Retail' (right column). Left column, bottom to top: Ecommerce, about 9% (red #EB483F, label 'Ecommerce' in white); Addressable retail, about 50% (mid grey #7F7F7F, label 'Addressable retail' in white), spanning about 9% to 59.5%; Non-addressable retail, about 40% (light grey #A6A6A6, label in white), spanning about 59.5% to 100%. Right column, bottom to top, all segments separated by thin white rules: about 8.5% Ecommerce red #EB483F; about 2% red #EB483F; about 1% red #EB483F; about 6% salmon #F3918C; about 6% salmon #F3918C; about 5% salmon #F3918C; about 1% salmon #F7B6B2; about 1% dark red #C0272D; about 1% pale salmon #F7B6B2 (near 40 to 42%); about 13% salmon #F4A3A0 region (about 42 to 54%) in #F7B6B2; about 1.5% #F7B6B2; about 3% pale pink #F4D3D3; about 15% pale pink #F4D3D3 (about 59.5 to 79%); about 12% salmon #F7B6B2 (about 79 to 91.5%); about 8% very pale pink #FBDAD9 (about 91.5 to 100%). No printed values are shown for any segment, so all sizes are estimates. No legend. No highlighted points. Segment labels appear only on the left column and the bottom red segment of the right column is unlabelled. No logos, icons or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands with a white #FFFFFF central panel. Flat, minimal design. Background is light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands, with a white #FFFFFF content panel between them. Title is set in a serif (Photina MT, regular weight) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle is the same serif at about 2.6% in #545454. Chart title and axis and labels use Helvetica Neue, the chart title bold and the rest regular, at about 1.9 to 2.2% of height. Body and footer text is Helvetica Neue regular at about 1.8% in #545454. Author name is Helvetica Neue bold in #A6A6A6, date and page number regular in #A6A6A6. Accents are red #EB483F and its tints (#F3918C, #F7B6B2, #FBDAD9) with a dark red #C0272D. Columns are flat rectangles with thin white separators, no shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 34

**Text**

Is that a ‘tech’ company?

Or is it a retailer using a new channel?

### Slide 36

**Text**

Front of mind: unbundling TV / YouTube launched in 2004 – a decade later, US TV finally unlocked / Annual change in US pay TV subscriptions / 2% / 0% / -2% / -4% / -6% / -8% / 2011 / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / Source: JP Morgan / Benedict Evans –– February 2020 / 36

**Layout**

Full-width horizontal band layout: a light grey header band (top 0 to 20.5% of height) holds the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%; a white content band (20.5% to 86.1%) holds a single line chart whose plot starts at about x 20.5% and runs to about x 95.5%; a light grey footer band (86.1% to 100%) holds the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the author, date and page number at right, with the page number ending at x 95.8%. Left margin for text is about 20% of slide width; there is no logo.

**Visuals**

Chart: line chart with markers, titled 'Annual change in US pay TV subscriptions'. Y-axis: percent, labelled 2%, 0%, -2%, -4%, -6%, -8%, with horizontal dotted gridlines at each label; range from -8% to about +3%; the bottom axis line sits at -8%. X-axis: years 2011 to 2019, one category per year, solid grey baseline. One series, drawn as a red line (about 3pt) with small red diamond markers at each year. Values are not printed; estimated values: 2011 about +0.2%, 2012 about +0.4%, 2013 about -0.1%, 2014 about -0.5%, 2015 about -1.9%, 2016 about -2.2%, 2017 about -3.7%, 2018 about -4.4%, 2019 about -7.3%. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the uniform markers; the steepest drop is between 2018 and 2019. Chart has no border and sits on the white band.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide, with a white #FFFFFF band behind the chart from 20.5% to 86.1% of slide height. Two-tone grey and white layout with a grey header and footer framing a white content area. Title in a serif face resembling Photina, regular weight, about 5.6% of slide height, colour #545454. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, about 2.6% of slide height, colour #545454. Chart title and axis labels in Helvetica Neue, chart title bold about 2.2% of slide height, axis labels regular about 1.85% of slide height, colour #545554. Footer text Helvetica Neue about 1.85%, colour #A6A6A6 with the author name in bold; source line in #545454. Accent colour is red #EF4136 for the series line and markers. Gridlines are thin dotted grey about #A6A6A6; baseline solid grey about #8C8C8C. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or borders.

### Slide 37

**Text**

US pay TV subs down 20% and falling / For generations, most of the US was sold a big and expensive pay TV bundle - this is now breaking apart / US pay TV subscriptions (m) / Incumbents / Netflix / 100 / 75 / 50 / 25 / 0 / 2011 / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / Source: JP Morgan, Netflix / Benedict Evans / –– February 2020 / 37

**Layout**

A light grey header band across the top holds the title and subtitle, both left-aligned to x 20.1%. A white content panel spans the full width between y 20.5% and y 86.1%, and inside it a single line chart fills x 20.5% to 95.5% with a legend in the left margin at x 4.8%. A light grey footer band below holds the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the author, date and page number at right, with the page number ending at about x 95.6% near y 91%.

**Visuals**

One line chart. Chart title: 'US pay TV subscriptions (m)' in bold, top-left above the plot. Y axis: subscriptions in millions, linear, range 0 to 100 (labelled 0, 25, 50, 75, 100) with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each 25 step. X axis: years 2011 to 2019, one category per year, with a solid thin grey baseline at 0. Series 1 'Incumbents' in red with diamond markers, values about: 2011 98, 2012 99, 2013 99, 2014 98, 2015 96, 2016 94, 2017 91, 2018 87, 2019 81 (no values printed; estimates). Series 2 'Netflix' in grey with diamond markers, values about: 2011 20, 2012 25, 2013 31, 2014 37, 2015 43, 2016 48, 2017 53, 2018 58, 2019 61 (no values printed; estimates). No data labels, no highlighted points. Legend at upper left of the chart, outside the plot, with red diamond 'Incumbents' above grey diamond 'Netflix'. Source of data printed in footer: JP Morgan, Netflix.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF panel from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Header and footer bands and page background are light grey #F1F1F1; the central content panel is white #FFFFFF. Title text is dark grey #545454 in a high-contrast serif (Photina-style, regular weight) at about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle is dark grey #545454 in the same serif, regular, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title, legend, axis labels and source line are dark grey #545454 in a neo-grotesque sans (Helvetica Neue) at about 1.9% of slide height; chart title is bold. Author name is bold #A6A6A6 and date and page number are regular #A6A6A6 in the same sans. Incumbents series accent red about #E94B3C with 2-3 px line and diamond markers; Netflix series grey about #A6A6A6 with matching line and diamond markers. Gridlines are thin dotted light grey about #BFBFBF. Baseline is a thin solid grey about #8C8C8C. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or borders.

### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- What would change the score: I would add direct labels and callouts on the key data points, for example the crossover on slide 38, so the headline claim is visible in the chart itself.

**Text**

And TV is changing a lot faster than retail
US cord-cutting % has passed ecommerce % in just 5 years
Digital change in US retail and TV
Cord cutting
Ecommerce
20%
15%
10%
5%
0%
2000
2005
2010

2019
Source: US Census, JP Morgan, @BenedictEvans
Benedict Evans — February 2020
38

**Layout**

Full-bleed light grey (#F1F1F1) slide split into three horizontal bands: a grey header band at top holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at about x 20.1% with the title baseline near 8% of height; a white central band (y 20.5% to 86.1%) holding the chart; and a grey footer band (y 86.1% to 100%) holding the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. Content sits on a left margin of about 20% of width with the right edge near 95%; the legend sits outside the left margin at x 4.8%. The page number 38 sits bottom right at x 94.4%.

**Visuals**

One line chart, titled 'Digital change in US retail and TV' (bold sans, left-aligned above the plot). Y axis: percentage from 0% to 20% in 5% steps, gridlines dotted light grey at each 5% step; no axis unit label beyond the % signs. X axis: years 2000 to 2019 (labels printed at 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015, 2019), one point per year. Series 1 'Cord cutting' is a red (#E8443A approx) line with red diamond markers, starting 2014 and running to 2019; values about 0.5% (2014), 2.4% (2015), 4.6% (2016), 8.1% (2017), 12.2% (2018), 18.6% (2019), all estimates as no values are printed. Series 2 'Ecommerce' is a grey (#A6A6A6 approx) line with grey diamond markers from 2000 to 2019; values about 1.2% (2000), 1.5% (2001), 1.9% (2002), 2.3% (2003), 2.8% (2004), 3.2% (2005), 3.8% (2006), 4.4% (2007), 4.5% (2008), 4.9% (2009), 5.5% (2010), 6.0% (2011), 6.7% (2012), 7.5% (2013), 8.3% (2014), 9.5% (2015), 10.7% (2016), 11.9% (2017), 12.8% (2018), 15.0% (2019); all estimates. Legend at upper left of the chart: red diamond 'Cord cutting' above grey diamond 'Ecommerce'. The 2019 red and grey points are the highlighted end points where cord cutting (about 18.6%) passes ecommerce (about 15.0%); the lines cross between 2018 and 2019. Horizontal baseline at 0% in mid grey.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF for the chart band. Title in PhotinaMT regular serif, colour #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in PhotinaMT regular serif, #545454, about 2.6% of height. Chart title in HelveticaNeue-Bold sans, #545554, about 2.2% of height. Axis labels, legend and source in HelveticaNeue regular sans, #545554 or #545454, about 1.85% of height. Footer author in HelveticaNeue-Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in HelveticaNeue regular #A6A6A6, about 1.85% of height. Accents: red #E8443A for cord cutting series, grey #A6A6A6 for ecommerce series. Lines are about 2 to 3 px thick with diamond markers; gridlines are thin dotted #BFBFBF-like grey; no shadows, gradients or corner radii.

### Slide 39

**Text**

Teen share down by half / Pay TV share of US teen viewing hours is down by half in three years / US teen daily video consumption share / Pay TV / Netflix / Youtube / 40% / 30% / 20% / 10% / 0% / Mar-16 / Sept-16 / Mar-17 / Sept-17 / Mar-18 / Sept-18 / Mar-19 / Sept-19 / Source: Piper Jaffray / Benedict Evans — February 2020 / 39

**Layout**

A full-width light grey header band holds the title and subtitle, left-aligned at x 20.1% with the title sitting above the subtitle. A white content area fills the middle with a line chart starting at x 20.5%, legend at the far left margin at x 4.8%, and a light grey footer band carries the source line at left and the author, date and slide number at right, with the slide number at x 94.4%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'US teen daily video consumption share', with three series drawn with diamond markers and straight segments. Y axis is percentage share from 0% to 40% in 10% steps, with dotted horizontal gridlines at 10%, 20%, 30% and 40%. X axis is categorical time points from Mar-16 to Sept-19 at six-month intervals (Mar-16, Sept-16, Mar-17, Sept-17, Mar-18, Sept-18, Mar-19, Sept-19). Pay TV (red #E8453C line and markers): about 26% Mar-16, about 25% Sept-16, about 23% Mar-17, about 22% Sept-17, about 20% Mar-18, about 16% Sept-18, about 14% Mar-19, about 12% Sept-19; values are estimates as the slide prints none. Netflix (light grey #A6A6A6 line and markers): about 38% Mar-16, about 37% Sept-16, about 38% Mar-17, about 37% Sept-17, about 39% Mar-18, about 38% Sept-18, about 37% Mar-19, about 35% Sept-19. Youtube (dark grey #545454 line and markers): about 23% Mar-16, about 26% Sept-16, about 26% Mar-17, about 29% Sept-17, about 30% Mar-18, about 33% Sept-18, about 32% Mar-19, about 37% Sept-19. Legend at left with diamond swatches: Pay TV (red), Netflix (grey), Youtube (dark grey). No points are highlighted. Chart has no visible axis titles beyond the chart title.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide fill, with a white #FFFFFF band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands framing a white #FFFFFF content area. Title in PhotinaMT, a serif, regular weight, colour #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in PhotinaMT regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title in HelveticaNeue-Bold, #545554, about 2.2%. Axis labels and legend in HelveticaNeue regular, #545554, about 1.85%. Footer source in HelveticaNeue #545454; author name in HelveticaNeue-Bold #A6A6A6, date and slide number in HelveticaNeue #A6A6A6, all about 1.85%. Series lines are straight, roughly 3 px wide, with diamond markers; dotted light grey gridlines; solid grey horizontal baseline at 0%. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Header band across the top 20.5% of the slide with the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%, sharing a left margin. The white content band spans 20.5% to 86.1% and holds the chart, which runs from a left axis at about x 18.6% to a right edge near x 95.1%. The grey footer band covers the bottom 13.9%, with the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the author, date and slide number at right, the number ending near x 95.6%.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart titled 'US annual content budgets, 2019 ($bn)'. Y axis runs from 0 to 20 in steps of 5 ($bn), with dotted horizontal gridlines at 5, 10, 15 and 20 and a solid baseline at 0. X axis lists ten companies left to right: Disney, Comcast/NBCU, Netflix, ViacomCBS, Warner, Amazon, Fox, Sony, Discovery, Apple. Two series: Entertainment (dark grey #7F8080, bottom segment) and Sport (light grey #A6A5A6, top segment). Netflix, Amazon and Apple columns are filled red #EC473F instead, and their Sport segments, if any, are light red. Values are estimates from the image (no values are printed): Disney Entertainment about 10.5, Sport about 7.7, total about 18.2; Comcast/NBCU Entertainment about 10.9, Sport about 5.7, total about 16.6; Netflix single red bar about 15.0; ViacomCBS Entertainment about 10.9, Sport about 1.9, total about 12.8; Warner Entertainment about 8.3, Sport about 2.4, total about 10.7; Amazon red about 7.1 with a thin light-red top to about 7.5; Fox Entertainment about 2.5, Sport about 4.3, total about 6.8; Sony Entertainment about 3.5, Sport about 0.3, total about 3.8; Discovery Entertainment about 3.0 only; Apple red about 2.1. Legend at upper left: Sport swatch above Entertainment swatch, stacked, on a white box. No highlighted points beyond the red columns.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF band behind the chart area. Flat, minimal look with a light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer band framing a white #FFFFFF content area. Title in a serif (Photina-like, regular weight) in charcoal #545454, about 5.56% of slide height, with the subtitle in the same serif at about 2.59%, also #545454. Chart title in bold sans-serif (Helvetica Neue Bold) #545554 at about 2.22%. Axis, category and legend labels in Helvetica Neue regular #545554 at about 1.85%. Footer text in Helvetica Neue #A6A6A6, the author name in bold. Accent colour is red #EC473F on the three highlighted columns. Columns have thin white separators between segments. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 41

**Text**

Streaming is a third of US TV production / A third of original series in the USA are now from Netflix, Amazon and other new entrants / US scripted original series / Online / Broadcast / Pay cable / Basic cable / 500 / 400 / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / 2009 / 2010 / 2011 / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / Source: FX Networks Research / Benedict Evans — February 2020 / 41

**Layout**

Full-slide light grey (#F1F1F1) frame with a white band across the middle holding a single stacked column chart, the title block left-aligned at about 20% from the left edge, and a footer row beneath. The title sits near the top, with the subtitle directly below it; the chart title sits above the plot, a vertical legend sits at the far left, and the source line and the presenter credit with slide number sit in the bottom grey band.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart titled US scripted original series (bold sans-serif, dark grey). Y axis: 0 to 500 scripted series count (units: number of series), gridlines at 100 intervals drawn as light grey dotted lines, labels 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500. X axis: calendar years 2009 to 2018, one column per year, labels in dark grey below the baseline. Series from bottom to top: Basic cable (mid grey #7F7F7F), Pay cable (grey #A6A6A6), Broadcast (light grey #BFBFBF), Online (red #EB483F). Each column has thin white separators between segments. Approximate values, not printed on slide: 2009 basic about 66, pay about 86, broadcast about 208 (total about 208, no online). 2010 basic about 74, pay about 100, broadcast about 211, online about 215. 2011 basic about 111, pay about 144, broadcast about 260, online about 265. 2012 basic about 124, pay about 154, broadcast about 273, online about 287. 2013 basic about 161, pay about 194, broadcast about 325, online about 348. 2014 basic about 173, pay about 209, broadcast about 356, online about 388. 2015 basic about 186, pay about 223, broadcast about 373, online about 422. 2016 basic about 183, pay about 219, broadcast about 366, online about 455. 2017 basic about 174, pay about 217, broadcast about 370, online about 490. 2018 basic about 145, pay about 190, broadcast about 335, online about 495. No points highlighted. Legend: vertical list at far left, top to bottom Online (red square), Broadcast (light grey square), Pay cable (grey square), Basic cable (dark grey square), all squares about 1.5% of slide height, labels in regular sans-serif. No logos, photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for the top and bottom bands with a white #FFFFFF band from about 20.5% to 86.1% of slide height. Title in PhotinaMT regular serif, dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height (cap height about 3.9%). Subtitle in PhotinaMT regular serif, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and legend and axis labels in Helvetica Neue (bold for chart title, regular for others), #545554, about 1.85 to 2.2% of slide height. Footer text Helvetica Neue regular #A6A6A6 for credit and slide number, source line #545454. Accent colour is the red #EB483F used only for the Online series. Lines are thin dotted light grey gridlines and a grey baseline. Shapes are flat, square-cornered, no shadows or gradients.

43

**Layout**

Two horizontal bands: a light grey header band across the top (about 0 to 20.5% of height) holding the title and subtitle, and a white content band (about 20.5 to 86.1%) holding the chart, with a light grey footer band below (about 86.1 to 100%). Title and subtitle are left-aligned at about x 20.1%, which is the left margin of the source line and chart; the chart's y-axis labels start at about x 17%. The source line sits bottom-left at about x 20.1% and the author credit, date and page number sit bottom-right, the page number right-aligned near x 95.6%.

**Visuals**

One line chart. Title printed above the plot: 'Monthly Twitch viewing hours (m)'. Y axis: 0 to 1,000 in linear steps labelled 0, 250, 500, 750, 1,000 (unit: millions of hours, per the chart title); faint dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled tick. X axis: monthly time from Sep-12 to Sep-19, labelled every 12 months (Sep-12, Sep-13, Sep-14, Sep-15, Sep-16, Sep-17, Sep-18, Sep-19), with a solid grey baseline. One series, a single red line of about 2.5 px width with no markers, showing monthly Twitch viewing hours. Values are not printed, so estimates are: Sep-12 about 55; Sep-13 about 160 to 190; Sep-14 about 240 to 290; Sep-15 about 360 to 440; Sep-16 about 360 to 600; Sep-17 about 530 to 720; Sep-18 about 810 to 870; late Sep-19 about 890 with a peak of about 1,000 near early 2019. A vertical solid grey line of about 1 px at Sep-14 (about x 40.0%) runs from the baseline up to just above the 1,000 gridline, annotated 'Amazon acquisition for $970m' just right of its top. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the annotated vertical marker. Source: Twitchmetrics.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a full-width white #FFFFFF band from about 20.5% to 86.1% of height behind the chart. Flat, minimal look. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1 and the content band is white #FFFFFF. Title is a regular-weight high-contrast serif (PhotinaMT-like, Photina/Times-style) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height (about 6.3 lines of text tall per title cap-height band). Subtitle is the same serif in #545454 at about 2.6%. Chart title and annotation are Helvetica Neue Bold / Regular sans-serif in #545454 at about 2.2%. Axis labels and footer text are Helvetica Neue Regular at about 1.85%; footer author name is Helvetica Neue Bold in #A6A6A6 and the rest of the footer credit and page number are Helvetica Neue Regular in #A6A6A6. Accent colour is red #E8453C for the data line. Gridlines are dotted light grey #B0B0B0 about 0.5 px. Baseline and vertical marker are solid grey #666666. No shadows, gradients, corner radii or rounded shapes.

**Layout**

Full-width header band (top 20.5% of slide) in light grey holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%, with a white middle band containing a single bar chart spanning roughly x 19% to 95%. A light grey footer band below the chart holds the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the author, date and page number at right, with the page number at x 94.4%. Margins are about 20% left and about 5% right; no logo.

**Visuals**

One vertical bar chart. Title above the plot: "Monthly viewing hours (bn)" in bold sans, left-aligned at x 20.5%. Y-axis: numeric labels 0 to 6 at the left, unit billions of viewing hours (bn), range 0 to 6 with major ticks every 1, horizontal dotted light-grey gridlines at each integer. X-axis: five categories with a solid grey baseline at 0. Series (single series, values printed nowhere, so estimates): Total UK TV about 5.8 bn (grey #7F7F7F); Netflix USA about 3.6 bn (grey #7F7F7F); BBC 1 (UK) about 1.1 bn (grey #7F7F7F); Global Twitch about 0.9 bn (highlighted red #EC4B3D, the only coloured bar); ITV 1 (UK) about 0.85 bn (grey #7F7F7F). No legend. Bars are flat, no shadows, evenly spaced, approximately 6% of slide width each, with the Global Twitch bar as the highlighted point.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide fill, with a white #FFFFFF band across the middle 65.6% of the height. **Layout**

Two horizontal bands frame the slide: a light grey header band at the top holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%, and a light grey footer band at the bottom holding the source line and the presenter credit with slide number. A white panel fills the middle band, containing a legend at the far left (x 4.8%) and a single full-width stacked column chart starting at x 20.5% with its chart title above the plot. The slide number sits at the right margin (x 94.4%, y 91.1%).

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart titled 'Netflix subscribers (m)' (bold, left-aligned above the plot). Y axis: 0 to 175 in steps of 25, unit millions (m), dotted light grey horizontal gridlines at each 25 step, labels right-aligned. X axis: 11 quarterly categories from Mar 17 to Sept 19, solid thin grey baseline at 0. Series, stacked bottom to top: North America (#7F7F7F grey), EMEA (red, about #E84A3C), LATAM (salmon, about #F29389), APAC (light pink, about #F6B5AE). Labelled values: none printed on the bars; estimated totals about 94, 99, 104, 111, 119, 125, 130, 139, 149, 151, 158. Estimated North America values: about 55, 56, 57, 59, 61, 62, 63, 65, 67, 67, 67. No highlighted points. Legend: top-to-bottom APAC, LATAM, EMEA, North America, each with a small square swatch, at the left of the chart, not on the plot. Bars are white-outlined thin separators between segments; bar width about 2.7% of slide width, evenly spaced.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF middle panel. Title colour dark grey #545454, subtitle #545454, chart title and axis/legend text #545554, source line #545454, footer name and date #A6A6A6, page number #A6A6A6. Title and subtitle in PhotinaMT, a serif with regular weight; chart title and presenter name in HelveticaNeue-Bold; all other labels in HelveticaNeue regular. Title text height about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, body and axis labels about 1.85%. Shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or gradients; bars have square corners and thin white separators; gridlines are dotted grey hairlines.

The footer sits bottom right below the panel, with the author name and date at left and the slide number at far right at about 94.4% x, aligned to the panel's right edge.

none

Quote and attribution in a serif (Photina-style, a transitional Times-like serif) in regular weight, coral red #EB483F, about 5.56% of slide height for the quote lines, same size for attribution. Footer in Helvetica Neue, about 1.85% of slide height, grey #A6A6A6; author name bold, date and slide number regular. Sans-serif footer, serif display text, no accent shapes.

### Slide 49

**Text**

Now: the great unbundling. Next: the great rebundling
For TV and retail, the old bundles are going, but we will get new ones
New distribution channels / break apart old / aggregation models
Everyone wants to / unbundle and go direct
But!
There can only be so many brand / relationships
