### Slide 53

**Text**

Today, anyone does anything online / In 2017, 40% of Americans met their partners online / US heterosexual couples who met online, by year of meeting / 40% / 30% / 20% / 10% / 0% / 1995 / 2000 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 2017 / Source: Stanford/GfK / Benedict Evans –– February 2020 / 53

**Layout**

Two-band slide: a light grey header band (top 20.5%) holds the left-aligned title and subtitle; a white content band (20.5% to 86.1%) holds the chart; a light grey footer band (below 86.1%) holds the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. Title and source share a left edge at about 20.1% of slide width, leaving a wide left margin of about 20% and a right margin of about 5.6%. The page number sits at the bottom right, aligned to the chart's right edge.

**Visuals**

One line chart, a single series, plotted as a red line with diamond markers at each year. Chart title: US heterosexual couples who met online, by year of meeting. Y axis: percentage of couples, 0% to 40%, gridlines at 10% intervals, labels 0%, 10%, 20%, 30%, 40%. X axis: year of meeting, 1995 to 2017, labels shown at 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015 and 2017. Values are not printed on the chart; estimated values (about) by year: 1995 about 1.5%, 1996 about 1.8%, 1997 about 2.8%, 1998 about 4.4%, 1999 about 6.7%, 2000 about 9.2%, 2001 about 11.6%, 2002 about 13.9%, 2003 about 15.9%, 2004 about 17.4%, 2005 about 18.6%, 2006 about 19.0%, 2007 about 19.2%, 2008 about 19.3%, 2009 about 20.4%, 2010 about 20.8%, 2011 about 22.2%, 2012 about 22.8%, 2013 about 25.6%, 2014 about 28.0%, 2015 about 31.8%, 2016 about 34.6%, 2017 about 38.3%. The line rises steeply from 1995 to 2000, flattens around 2005 to 2009 near 19 to 20%, then rises steeply to 2017. No legend, no highlighted points, no annotations. Source attributed on slide: Stanford/GfK.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide background with a white band #FFFFFF across the middle. Light grey header and footer bands (#F1F1F1) framing a white chart band (#FFFFFF). Title in a light serif (PhotinaMT-style, regular weight) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, about 2.6% of slide height, #545454. Chart title and axis labels in a neo-grotesque sans (Helvetica Neue; chart title bold, labels regular) about 1.85 to 2.2% of slide height, dark grey #545554. Footer text in Helvetica Neue about 1.85% in #A6A6A6, author name bold. Accent: red line and markers, approximately #EE4036. Gridlines are thin dotted light grey, approximately #A6A6A6; the x axis baseline is a thin solid grey line, approximately #7F7F7F. No shadows, gradients, or rounded corners.

### Slide 54

**Text**

Sharon Stone
@sharonstone
I went on the @bumble dating sight and they closed my account. 👁 👁
Some users reported that it couldn’t possibly be me!
Hey @bumble, is being me exclusionary ? 🙎
Don’t shut me out of the hive 🐝
12:38 am · 30 Dec 2019 · Twitter for iPhone
4K Retweets 43.1K Likes
Benedict Evans
–– February 2020
54

**Layout**

A single white tweet-style card is centred horizontally in the upper-middle of the slide, spanning about 20% to 73% of the width and 21% to 72% of the height, with generous empty grey margins on all sides. There is no title. A small footer line sits in the bottom-right corner at about 91% of the height, with the author name, a dash and date, then the slide number at the far right, all on one baseline.

**Visuals**

One raster screenshot of a Twitter post, placed as a boxed rectangle (not full-bleed, not cropped) on the grey background. Subject: a post by the account Sharon Stone (@sharonstone), verified, showing a round black-and-white portrait photo of a woman with short tousled hair as avatar. Text in the post reads: 'I went on the @bumble dating sight and they closed my account.' followed by two blue eye emoji, then 'Some users reported that it couldn’t possibly be me!', then 'Hey @bumble, is being me exclusionary ?' followed by a blonde person shrugging emoji, then 'Don’t shut me out of the hive' followed by a bee emoji. Below the text: '12:38 am · 30 Dec 2019 · Twitter for iPhone', a thin grey divider line, then '4K Retweets' and '43.1K Likes'. Flat rendering, no lighting, no shadow. Dominant colours: white card, near-black text, Twitter blue #1DA1F2 for the handles and link, grey #657786 for secondary text. There is a small downward chevron at the top right of the card. No overlay or tint. No logos.

**Design**

Background is a flat light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient or pattern. The tweet card is white #FFFFFF with square corners and no visible shadow. Post text and author name are near-black #14171A in a Helvetica Neue style sans-serif, regular weight for body and bold for the name; body text is roughly 3.6% of slide height. Handle and secondary text are grey #657786; mentions and link are blue #1DA1F2; verified badge is blue #1DA1F2. Footer text is Helvetica Neue, the name in bold and the rest in regular, both #A6A6A6 at about 1.85% of slide height. Thin light grey divider line inside the card.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 20.1, y 20.9, w 52.7, h 50.6 [#FFFFFF]: Raster screenshot of a Twitter post by Sharon Stone (@sharonstone) with verified badge, round black-and-white avatar portrait, post text with @bumble mentions and emoji (two blue eyes, shrugging blonde person, bee), timestamp '12:38 am · 30 Dec 2019 · Twitter for iPhone', and '4K Retweets 43.1K Likes'. 1012 by 546 pixels. (Boxed rectangular image, not cropped, not full-bleed; white card background #FFFFFF, square corners, no shadow, no overlay or tint; sits on the #F1F1F1 background.)

### Slide 55

**Text**

Models for market expansion
Penetration and consumer acceptance drives expansion
Vertical integration
Horizontal expansion
The full stack’ model: from / booking.com to Airbnb
Taking established online / models to new segments
Benedict Evans
–– February 2020
55

**Layout**

Header band on a light grey field holds the left-aligned title and subtitle at the top-left, starting at about 20% of slide width. A full-width white band fills the middle from about 20.5% to 86% of slide height, divided into three columns by thin light grey gutters at about 20% and 58% of width; the left column is empty and the two right columns each hold a centred heading, a two-line statement and a red double-headed arrow. A grey footer strip at the bottom right carries the author name, date and page number, with no margin on the left of the footer.

**Visuals**

Two red double-headed dotted arrows, both with solid triangular arrowheads. Arrow 1 is vertical, in the left content column (centre about x 24%), spanning from about y 35% to y 77%, pointing up at the top and down at the bottom. Arrow 2 is horizontal, below the Horizontal expansion text (centre about y 56%), spanning from about x 63% to x 86%, with arrowheads at both ends. No photographs, charts or logos.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with a white central band #FFFFFF. Title and body text in dark grey #545454; footer text in mid grey #A6A6A6; accents are red dotted arrows (about #E8413A). Title and body use PhotinaMT, a transitional serif, regular weight; column headings use PhotinaMT-SemiBold. Footer uses HelveticaNeue (Bold for the author name, regular for date and page number), a neo-grotesque sans-serif. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, column headings and body about 3.3%, footer about 1.9%. Lines are thin dotted strokes with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 58

**Text**

Growth and global diffusion / More and more investment in company creation around the world / Venture capital investments ($bn) / Rest of World / USA / 350 / 300 / 250 / 200 / 150 / 100 / 50 / 0 / 2004 / 2005 / 2006 / 2007 / 2008 / 2009 / 2010 / 2011 / 2012 / 2013 / 2014 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / Source: NVCA / Benedict Evans / –– February 2020 / 58

**Layout**

Two horizontal bands: a light grey header band (top 0 to 20.5%) holding the title and subtitle, and a light grey footer band (bottom 13.9%) on a white middle band (20.5% to 86.1%). Title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 20.1%, the chart is in a plot area from about x 18% to 95%, the source line sits at x 20.1% and the footer name, date and page number are right-aligned in the bottom band with the page number at x 94.4%.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked vertical bar chart titled 'Venture capital investments ($bn)' in bold Helvetica Neue, #545554. Y axis from 0 to 350 in steps of 50, labels in Helvetica Neue regular #545454, with light grey dotted horizontal gridlines at each step. X axis years 2004 to 2019, one label per bar, with a thin grey baseline at 0. Two series stacked per bar: USA in grey #808080 at the bottom and Rest of World in red #EA4A3C on top, with thin white separators between segments. Bars are about 35 px wide and evenly spaced. Values are not printed; estimates (about, in $bn, total bar height): 2004 about 26 (USA about 22); 2005 about 30 (USA about 24); 2006 about 37 (USA about 30); 2007 about 46 (USA about 37); 2008 about 50 (USA about 37); 2009 about 36 (USA about 27); 2010 about 47 (USA about 32); 2011 about 65 (USA about 45); 2012 about 61 (USA about 41); 2013 about 72 (USA about 49); 2014 about 114 (USA about 72); 2015 about 151 (USA about 80); 2016 about 159 (USA about 77); 2017 about 183 (USA about 86); 2018 about 309 (USA about 142); 2019 about 257 (USA about 134). Legend at top left of chart, stacked vertically: red square with 'Rest of World' above grey square with 'USA', squares about 1.5% of width. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands with white #FFFFFF middle band. Minimalist, flat, no decoration. Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands with white #FFFFFF content band. Title and subtitle in Photina serif, regular weight, title #545454 and subtitle #545454. Chart title and axis labels in Helvetica Neue, bold for chart title and regular for others, #545454. Footer text in Helvetica Neue regular #A6A6A6, author name bold. Accent colour red #EA4A3C used for Rest of World series. Title height about 5.6% of slide height; body and axis text about 1.85% of slide height. Thin hairline gridlines and baseline, no shadows, no gradients, square bar corners.

### Slide 59

**Text**

Expanding software and tools from / work to everyday life / Software, automation, workflow, and tools… / From work and big business to everyone’s lives / From mainframes, to LinkedIn, to Tinder and Rigup / Benedict Evans / –– January 2020 / 59

**Layout**

Two-column split: a light grey text column occupies the left half and a black-and-white photograph fills the right half, full-height and full-bleed. The title sits top-left of the text column at about 24% from the top, with three left-aligned body lines below it on a common left margin of about 4.6%. The footer strip runs along the bottom of the text column, with the author name and date at left and the slide number at far right of the text column.

**Visuals**

One black-and-white photograph occupying the right half of the slide (x 28.1% to 100%, full height, full-bleed, cropped to fill the area). It shows a 1960s-style open-plan office under a grid of rectangular fluorescent ceiling lights in strong perspective, with rows of seated office workers at desks and telephones, and a standing man in the background. In the foreground right, a man in a dark suit and dark tie sits at a desk with his right hand pressed against his brow and eyes closed, and his left hand clasped over a document. In the foreground left, a large grey typewriter-style machine with the lettering 'FRIDEN' is visible, and a small pale rectangular object and a grey stapler-like object sit on the desk at bottom right. Lighting is flat fluorescent overhead; the image is monochrome grey with white highlights and dark blacks. No chart, table, logo or icon.

**Design**

Background light grey #F2F2F2 for the left column, with the photograph filling the right half. Title and body text in dark grey #545454, set in a serif typeface resembling Photina (a transitional Roman serif), regular weight. Title size about 4.5% of slide height, body lines about 3.3% of slide height. Footer text in light grey #A6A6A6, Helvetica Neue style sans-serif, bold for the author name and regular for the date and number, about 1.85% of slide height. No lines, shapes, shadows or gradients visible.

### Slide 62

**Text**

Can there be another massive increase in scale? / Once you’ve connected everyone, how do you create a bigger market? / ? / Global installed base, 2019 (bn) / 6 / 5 / 4 / 3 / 2 / 1 / 0 / Mainframes / PCs in 1994 / Web in 2007 / Smartphones in 2019 / Global population over 14 / Source: UN, GSMA, ITU, Apple, Google, CNNIC, @BenedictEvans / Benedict Evans –– February 2020 / 62

**Layout**

Full-width slide with a light grey header band (top 0 to 20.5%) holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%, a white content area from 20.5% to 86.1% with a bar chart whose axis starts at x 19.2% and runs to about 95.3%, and a light grey footer band from 86.1% to 100% holding the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the author, date and page number at right, with the page number ending at about x 95.6%. No logo is present; there is no visible slide-number box beyond the footer text.

**Visuals**

Bar chart, vertical columns. Title printed above the chart: 'Global installed base, 2019 (bn)'. Y axis: unit bn (billions), range 0 to 6, gridlines and labels at every whole unit 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6; no axis title. X axis: five categories, labelled Mainframes, PCs in 1994, Web in 2007, Smartphones in 2019, Global population over 14. No data labels are printed on the bars, so values are estimates: Mainframes shows no visible bar (about 0); PCs in 1994 about 0.1 (sliver bar); Web in 2007 about 1.2; Smartphones in 2019 about 4.0 (highlighted bar in red); Global population over 14 about 5.2. No legend. Highlighted points: the Smartphones bar is coloured red #EB483F while the other bars are grey #7F7F7F. Annotation: a red dotted curved arrow with a solid filled arrowhead rises from just above the Smartphones bar (about x 72.5%, y 46.7%) to the top of the Global population bar (arrowhead about x 83.3%, y 41.1%), with a red question mark above it at about x 76.4%, y 34.6%. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from 20.5% to 86.1% of the height. Flat, minimal style. Header and footer bands are light grey #F1F1F1; the content area is white #FFFFFF. Title text is dark grey #545454 in a high-contrast serif (PhotinaMT, regular weight) at about 5.6% of slide height, roughly 14 times the body size. Subtitle is #545454 in the same serif, regular, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title is bold sans-serif (Helvetica Neue Bold) #545554 at about 2.2%. Axis tick labels and category labels are Helvetica regular #545554 at about 1.9%. Source line is Helvetica Neue regular #545454 at about 1.9%. Footer author name is Helvetica Neue Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number Helvetica Neue regular #A6A6A6, all about 1.9%. Accent colour is red #EB483F, used for the highlighted bar, the arrow and the question mark. Bars are solid flat fills with no outline, no shadows, no gradients and square corners. Gridlines are thin dotted light grey lines spanning the plot width at each unit value. Baseline at zero is a thin solid grey line.

### Slide 63

**Text**

And is this the new thing, or part of the old thing?
Smartphones began as a PC accessory, but now PCs are a smartphone accessory
Stupid
Exciting!
Boring
PC
internet
Mobile?
?
?
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
63

**Layout**

Full-width title block at the top with the title left-aligned at about x 20 and the subtitle directly beneath it, both sharing one left edge. Below sits a white chart band from about y 20.5% to 86% spanning the full slide width, divided into vertical columns by thin light-grey gutters; the columns are headed Stupid, Exciting! and Boring, with the fourth unlabelled column holding the diagram. The footer line sits near the bottom, right-aligned at about x 75 to 95, with the page number at about x 94.4.

**Visuals**

Line chart-style diagram with no axes or numbers. A single grey (#7F7F7F) curve, about 0.3% of slide height thick, starts at the bottom-left of the white band (about x 20.5, y 84.7), stays nearly flat, then rises in an S-shape through the Exciting! column and reaches the PC internet circle at its lower-left edge. From the PC internet circle a solid grey arrow with a filled triangular head points right toward about x 76, y 40.5. Three dotted grey arrows fan up and right from the arrow's tip toward the top of the fourth column. A red (#EB483F) arrow runs from the Mobile? circle up-left to the lower-left of the PC internet circle, with a red question mark at about x 77.4, y 41.7 beside the grey arrow tip. A second red arrow runs from the lower-right of the Mobile? circle down-right to the top-left of the question-mark circle, with a red question mark at about x 82.7, y 62.6. The Mobile? circle is a white-filled circle with a red outline about 0.3% thick. The PC internet circle is a solid #7F7F7F filled circle. The question-mark circle is a solid #7F7F7F filled circle with a dotted grey outline, and a dotted grey line leaves its right edge and exits the slide at the right at about y 75. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 solid, with a white #FFFFFF band from about y 20.5% to 86%. Light grey #F1F1F1 background framing the slide, with a white chart band. Title and subtitle in a dark grey #545454 serif (PhotinaMT-style), title about 5.6% of slide height and subtitle about 2.6%. Column labels in the same serif at about 3.0% in #545454. Diagram labels in Helvetica, #FFFFFF on grey circles and #545454 for Mobile?. Question marks in red #EB483F, serif. Footer in HelveticaNeue at about 1.9%, #A6A6A6, with Benedict Evans in bold. Lines are thin and flat with no shadows, gradients or corner radii beyond the fully round circles. Column gutters are light grey #F1F1F1 strips about 0.4% wide.

### Slide 67

**Text**

Governments of the Industrial World, you / weary giants of flesh and steel, I come from / Cyberspace, the new home of Mind. On behalf / of the future, I ask you of the past to leave us / alone. You are not welcome among us. You / have no sovereignty where we gather.
- John Perry Barlow, 1996
Benedict Evans –– February 2020
67

**Layout**

A single white rectangular panel sits on a light grey full-slide background, offset to the right with its left edge at 17.4% and right edge at 95.6% of slide width, spanning 24.9% to 86.1% of slide height. Quote text is left-aligned inside the panel with a left inset of about 2.6% of slide width, and the attribution sits below a blank line at the lower left of the panel. The footer runs along the bottom right at about 91% of slide height: 'Benedict Evans' in bold, then '–– February 2020', then the slide number 67 right-aligned at about 94.4% to 95.6% of slide width, all sitting on the same baseline as the panel's right edge.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

No legend. Highlighted points: none beyond the colour differences; the first bar is grey, the three following bars are red and pale pink. Bars are solid rectangles with no outlines, evenly spaced.

Accent colours: red #EB483F, pale pink #FBDAD9, neutral grey #7F7F7F. Flat design with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

X axis: categories Apple, Microsoft, Google, Amazon, Facebook, Berkshire Hathaway, Alibaba, Tencent, Johnson & Johnson, Exxon, with a solid thin grey baseline at 0. Series (one series, values are estimates since no values are printed; marked about): Apple about 1,160; Microsoft about 1,140; Google about 900; Amazon about 860; Facebook about 560; Berkshire Hathaway about 530; Alibaba about 480; Tencent about 400; Johnson & Johnson about 360; Exxon about 295. No legend. No highlighted points beyond the colour coding.

### Slide 70

**Text**

Now we have issues. So many issues
Every month there’s something else to worry about
Hate speech
Encryption
Filter bubbles
Political
advertising
Elections
CSAM
Privacy
Radicalisation
Abuse of
market power
Ride
share
Face
recognition
AI
Bias
Fake News
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
70

**Layout**

Single-column composition on a plain light-grey field. The title sits top-left at about x 20% and y 5.5%, with the subtitle directly beneath it at y 13%, both left-aligned to the same edge at x 20.1%. A cluster of circles fills the middle band from about y 23% to y 86%, spanning x 20% to 93%, with no grid. The footer runs along the bottom right at y 91%: bold 'Benedict Evans', then '— February 2020', then the page number 70 at x 94.4%. The left fifth of the slide is empty.

**Visuals**

Packed-circle (bubble) diagram of 33 circles with no axes or legend. Eighteen circles carry labels: Hate speech, Encryption, Filter bubbles, Political advertising, Elections, CSAM, Privacy, Radicalisation, Abuse of market power, Ride share, Face recognition, AI Bias, Fake News. Two circles, Elections and Privacy, are full-strength red (#EB483F) and are the largest; they are the highlighted points. All other circles are #EB483F at 50% opacity, so they render as pale salmon on the #F1F1F1 ground. Labelled circles are sized roughly by importance, from about 14.5% of slide width (Elections, Privacy) down to about 7.9%. Unlabelled small circles of about 2.2% to 5.5% width are scattered among them. Each circle has a white outline of about 2 px. The circles touch or nearly touch and do not overlap. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a flat light grey, #F1F1F1, with no gradient or texture. Title is set in PhotinaMT, a regular-weight transitional serif, in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height (the cap height is about 0.7 of that). Subtitle is PhotinaMT regular in #545454 at about 2.6% of slide height. Bubble labels are PhotinaMT regular in white #FFFFFF at about 3.0% of slide height, with Elections and Privacy at about 3.3%. Footer is Helvetica Neue: 'Benedict Evans' in bold and the rest in regular, both at about 1.9% of slide height and coloured #A6A6A6. Bubbles are flat fills with no shadow, no border radius, and a thin white stroke. Accent colour is #EB483F, shown at full strength or at 50% opacity.

### Slide 71

**Text**

And this won’t get simpler
None of these people exist: these faces are algorithmically generated by a machine learning system
Benedict Evans — February 2020 71

**Layout**

Title and subtitle are left-aligned at about 20% from the left edge, near the top, with the title above the subtitle. A 4 by 2 grid of eight square-cropped portrait photos fills the middle-right of the slide, spanning about 20% to 94% horizontally and 21% to 86% vertically, with no visible gutters beyond thin light seams. The left margin area below the title is empty. The footer sits at about 91% from the top, right-aligned: author name bold, em dash and date, then the page number 71 at the far right.

**Visuals**

Eight photographs of computer-generated human faces, arranged in two rows of four, each shown as a rectangular tile of the same size (18.3% by 32.6% of the slide). Top row, left to right: a smiling man with short brown hair and a dark grey collared shirt against a taupe background; a man with dark wavy hair and a slight closed-mouth smile, blue-grey collar, grey wall background; a man with curly brown hair and thin wire-rimmed glasses, slight smile, navy jacket, blurred tan and green background; a smiling man with dark swept-back hair, navy collar, brown background. Bottom row, left to right: a balding man with a grey beard and smile, dark jacket, brown background; a young person with short dark hair and a neutral expression, pale grey top, sandy textured background; a smiling young man with short brown hair, grey collar, beige background; a smiling woman with long brown hair and bangs, light top, blurred green background. Framing is head and shoulders, front-facing, eyes at about the same height in each tile, lit evenly with soft frontal light. Dominant colours are warm browns, beige, taupe, navy and grey, with green in one background. Tiles are full rectangles with no borders, rounded corners or shadows, and no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Plain light grey background with no gradient or texture. Title and subtitle in a dark grey serif (Photina-style, roman weight), colour #545454. Title is large, cap height about 5.6% of slide height; subtitle about 2.6% of slide height. Footer text in Helvetica Neue, light grey #A6A6A6: author name in bold, date and page number in regular, about 1.85% of slide height. Photo tiles are square-edged, unbordered and butt together with hairline light gaps. No accent colours.

### Slide 73

**Text**

‘Break them up’ – the Brexit of tech
Every complex question has a simple answer
Break them up
Brexit
Into what?
And which
problems would
that solve?
Benedict Evans — February 2020
73

**Layout**

Left-aligned title and subtitle block starting at about 20% from the left edge near the top, with a centred three-part row beneath: a red circle, a grey arrow pointing right, a centred text block, a grey arrow pointing left, and a red circle on the right. The footer sits bottom right, with the author name and date followed by the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Two solid red circles of equal size (#EB483F), each about 20.7% of slide width and 36.8% of slide height, sitting at the same vertical level. The left circle contains the words 'Break them up' in white; the right circle contains 'Brexit' in white. Between the circles are two flat grey block arrows (#BFBFBF), each about 4% wide and 10.4% tall: the left arrow points right, from the left circle toward the centre text; the right arrow points left, from the right circle toward the centre text. The centre text reads 'Into what?' then a blank line then 'And which problems would that solve?' in dark grey. No chart, photograph or logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1. Plain light grey background (#F1F1F1) with no gradient or texture. Titles and body text in dark grey #545454 in a Photina-style serif (MT Photina), regular weight. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle and centre text about 2.6% to 3.3%. Accent colour is red #EB483F for the circles and grey #BFBFBF for the arrows. Footer in grey #A6A6A6, Helvetica Neue, author name bold, about 1.85% of slide height. Circles are flat with no shadow or outline; arrows are flat block shapes with no outline.

### Slide 74

**Text**

Coverage of real issues is accompanied by a steady flow of stories that don’t stand up to scrutiny
‘Google makes $4.7bn / from Google News!’
Extrapolated from a / 2008 guess of ‘$100m?’
‘The NHS gave Amazon / our health data!’
Alexa can read bits of the / public website to you
‘Amazon has private label!’
Like all retailers for a / century
Benedict Evans — February 2020
74

Footer in Helvetica Neue, name bold, date and number regular, about 1.9% of slide height. Thin flat rectangles, no rounded corners, no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 75

**Text**

Moral panic: Amazon does private label
'Well done – you just discovered retail’
Private Label as % revenue, 2018
40%
30%
20%
10%
0%
Amazon
Walmart
US groceries
Macy's
US general retail
UK packaged food
Source: Morgan Stanley, Euromonitor
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
75

**Layout**

Full-width slide split into three horizontal bands: a light grey header band (top 0 to about 20.5% of height) holding the title and subtitle, a white body band (about 20.5% to 86% of height) holding the bar chart, and a light grey footer band (about 86% to 100%) holding the source line at left and the author, date and page number at right. Title and subtitle are left-aligned at x about 20.1%, which is the common left margin for the source line; the chart title and axis labels sit at x about 17.5% to 20.5%. The chart occupies x about 17.5% to 95% and y about 27% to 84%. The page number 75 sits bottom right at x about 94.4%, aligned to the right margin of the chart gridlines.

**Visuals**

Bar chart (vertical columns). Title printed above the plot: 'Private Label as % revenue, 2018'. Y axis: percentage, range 0% to 40%, gridlines and labels at 0%, 10%, 20%, 30%, 40%; no axis title. X axis: categories, no axis title, labels Amazon, Walmart, US groceries, Macy's, US general retail, UK packaged food. Single series, no legend, no printed data values; values are estimates: Amazon about 1%, Walmart about 15%, US groceries about 19%, Macy's about 20%, US general retail about 23%, UK packaged food about 36%. Highlighted point: the Amazon column is coloured red (about #EE4035) while all other columns are mid grey #7F7F7F. Gridlines are light grey dotted lines at each 10% step. Baseline at 0% is a thin solid grey line.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF for the central chart band. Title in a high-contrast serif (Photina-style, Photina MT) regular weight, colour #545454, large, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height, set in straight single-style quotation marks. Chart title and axis labels in Helvetica Neue, chart title bold #545554 about 2.2% of height; axis and category labels Helvetica Neue regular #545554 about 1.85% of height. Source line Helvetica Neue regular #545454 about 1.85%. Footer author name Helvetica Neue bold #A6A6A6, date and page number Helvetica Neue regular #A6A6A6, about 1.85%. Bars are flat solid fills with no outline, no shadows, no gradients, square corners. Gridlines are 0.5 pt dotted light grey. No logos, no decorative rules.

### Slide 76

**Text**

But panics have causes, and
consequences
“Industrial food is a good thing, but what’s in the sausages?” - The
Jungle, 1906
Maybe we should break up ‘the beef trust’…
But we also need an FDA, ingredient lists, veterinary inspections,
hygiene rules…
Benedict Evans
–– January 2020
76

**Layout**

Two-column split at the slide's vertical midline: a text column occupies the left half and a full-height photograph fills the right half, bleeding off the top, right and bottom edges. The title sits top-left at about 24% of the slide height, with a left margin of about 4.6% of the slide width. The body text is left-aligned below it in three paragraphs. The footer line (author, date) and the page number 76 sit at the bottom, about 91% down the slide; the footer's position overlaps the photograph.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a full-colour printed caricature, in the style of an early-20th-century lithograph, cropped to a tall rectangle covering the right half of the slide and running full-height and full-bleed off the top, right and bottom edges. Subject: a grinning, heavyset man in a white chef's cap with a skull-and-crossbones emblem and a white short-sleeved shirt printed 'The Beef Trust', leaning forward with both hands on a counter. On the counter are a tied-off cooked ham labelled 'DEODORIZED HAM', sausages labelled 'EMBALMED', 'SAUSAGES' and similar, stacked tins labelled 'CORN…' and 'CORN BEEF', and a black cat with white chest and whiskers at right. Behind him, shelves hold jars and tins labelled 'CHEMICAL', 'CORN BEEF', 'BOB VEAL CHIC…', 'Tuberculosis Lard', and a hanging sign reading 'Putrefied Pork' is partly cut off at the right edge. Composition: the figure is centred in the frame, slightly right of centre, cropped at the top by the shelves and at the right edge by the frame. Lighting: flat, even, with no strong directional light. Dominant colours: cream and off-white, salmon and pink flesh tones, muted grey-green and tan shadows, with brick-red and orange tins. No overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F2F2F2 on the text side; photograph occupies the right half over a white #FFFFFF base. Left panel background is light grey #F2F2F2 and the photograph sits on a white #FFFFFF base. Title text is dark grey #545454 in a regular-weight serif (PhotinaMT, a Photina-style Times-like serif), about 4.5% of slide height, set in sentence case with no capitals other than the first word. Body text is dark grey #545454 in the same serif at about 2.5% of slide height with generous line spacing (about 1.6 times). Footer and page number are light grey #A6A6A6 in Helvetica Neue, about 1.85% of slide height; the author name is bold. There are no rules, cards, rounded corners, shadows or gradients, and no accent colour.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

Different types of problem have fundamentally different solutions

Privacy, security / (but not pricing)
Bad people / using tech
Abuse, fake news, / elections…
Benedict Evans — February 2020

Full-width light grey header band (top about 20% of the slide) holds a left-aligned serif title at x 20.1% with a subtitle beneath it; a white body band fills the middle (y 20.5% to 86.1%) containing a legend at far left and a single 100% stacked column chart with its chart title above the plot area. Vertical axis: 0% to 100%, labelled at 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%, with dotted horizontal gridlines at each 25% step; no axis title. Horizontal axis: four categories, Search, Social (DAUs), Online advertising, All advertising; no axis title. Each column totals 100%. Legend at upper left of the chart, listed top to bottom: Other (grey swatch), Facebook (pale pink swatch), Google (red swatch). Online advertising: Google about 46%, Facebook about 23%, Other about 31%. All advertising: Google about 25%, Facebook about 13%, Other about 62%. Colours: Google #EC473F red, Facebook #F9DAD9 pale pink, Other #7F8080 mid grey. Thin white separator lines between stacked segments. No highlighted points. No logos, photographs or icons.

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Multiple market-share bars for the same company show how one number changes with the market definition, which is the slide's point.

81

**Layout**

Full-width slide with a light grey (#F1F1F1) header band across the top and a light grey footer band at the bottom, with a white content band between them from about 20.5% to 86.1% of the slide height. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at about x 20.1% in the header band; the chart is a single vertical bar chart starting at about x 17% and ending at about x 93%, with its title above the plot. The source line sits left-aligned at x 20.1% in the footer, and the author, date and slide number sit right-aligned at the right margin (ending at about x 95.6%) on the same line.

**Visuals**

One vertical bar chart titled 'Apple market shares, 2019'. Y axis: 0% to 100% in 25% steps, labelled 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each step; no axis title. X axis: six categories with a thin grey baseline. Series (single series, bar heights are measured from the file, values about where not printed): Global unit sales, red bar, about 15%; Global installed base, grey bar, about 24%; US installed base, grey bar, about 65%; US app store sales, grey bar, about 75%; US teenager installed base, grey bar, about 80%; iOS app stores, red bar, about 100%. Highlighted points: the first (Global unit sales) and last (iOS app stores) bars are red #EB483F while the four middle bars are grey #7F7F7F. No legend. Bars are about 6.2% of slide width each, evenly spaced, with the iOS app stores bar reaching the 100% gridline. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

### Slide 82

**Text**

But they don’t have / market dominance! / Regulators define / the relevant market / however they like! / Market definition is everything / Regulators make their own decision on what the ‘relevant / market’ is for anti-trust purposes / IBM had 100% of the IBM mainframe market / Apple has 100% of the iPhone app store market / Benedict Evans –– January 2020 / Benedict Evans –– February 2020 / 82

**Layout**

Two-column split: the left half is a flat light-grey panel holding the title and three body paragraphs, with a left margin of about 4.6% of slide width. The right half is white and holds a boxed comic illustration occupying the top-right area from about 7.7% to 90.7% of slide height, with a white strip along the bottom. The title sits at about 24% from top, roughly mid-height on the left panel, and body text follows below it with even spacing. The footer line runs along the bottom of the right half, with the name and date left-aligned near x 74.9% and the page number '82' at the far right near x 94.4%.

**Visuals**

One comic-style illustration (a pop-art panel from the Batman slapping Robin meme) fills the right half in a rectangular box with a thick black border. Batman, at the right, with blue cowl and white-rimmed eye mask, leans in and slaps the back of Robin's head; Robin, seen from behind with black hair in a bun, wears a yellow top and has a green-gloved hand raised at the left edge and a blue-gloved hand at the lower left. Motion lines show the impact near Robin's head. Two cream speech bubbles with black outlines sit at the top of the panel, one from Robin's side and one from Batman's side, each containing comic text. Background of the panel is a saturated red. Lighting is flat and even, as in printed comics. Framing is a medium close-up, cropped at the frame edges, boxed rather than full-bleed. Dominant colours: red #D9372B, yellow #E8B82E, teal-blue #2F6F7E, cream #F5E6B8, black outlines.

**Design**

Background: Left half solid light grey #F2F2F2 and right half solid white #FFFFFF, with the illustration placed on the white half. Left panel background light grey #F2F2F2; right panel white #FFFFFF. Title in a light serif (Photina MT style) regular weight, colour grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Body text in the same serif, regular weight, colour grey #545454, about 3.0% of slide height, with generous line spacing within the first paragraph. Speech-bubble text is Comic Sans MS style, colour #323232, about 3.7% of slide height. Footer text is Helvetica Neue style in light grey #A6A6A6, about 1.9% of slide height, with the name in bold. No gradients, shadows or corner radii; the image has a sharp black frame.

### Slide 84

**Text**

But what kinds of competition would breakups affect?
Breakups would affect ad competition, but not product competition – unlikely to create a flood of YouTube alternatives
More competition / in the ad market
Split apart / Instagram or / YouTube
Little impact on / network effects / or product / competition
Benedict Evans — February 2020
84

**Layout**

Slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle block running across the top from about x 20% to 92%, with three circles arranged in a horizontal row across the middle of the slide, separated by a grey left arrow and a grey not-equal sign. Footer credit and slide number sit bottom right, aligned to the right margin near y 91%.

**Visuals**

Three circular shapes and two connector symbols. Left: a white circle with a thin red (#EB483F) outline, about 21.7% wide and 38.5% tall, containing the text 'More competition in the ad market'. Centre: a solid red (#EB483F) filled circle of the same size containing white text 'Split apart Instagram or YouTube'. Right: a circle with a thin dotted grey outline (about #808080), no fill, containing the text 'Little impact on network effects or product competition'. Between the left and centre circles is a solid light grey (#BFBFBF) block left-pointing arrow. Between the centre and right circles is a solid light grey (#BFBFBF) not-equal sign (≠). No photographs, charts, or logos.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Title in a serif typeface resembling Photina, regular weight, dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif family, regular weight, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Circle text in the same serif, regular weight, about 3.3% of slide height; white on the red circle, #545454 on the others. Footer in a sans-serif resembling Helvetica Neue: 'Benedict Evans' bold, the rest regular, #A6A6A6, about 1.85% of slide height. Shapes are flat with no shadows, corner radius full circle. Outlines: left circle solid red about 0.3% stroke; right circle dotted grey stroke about 0.4%.

88

**Layout**

Full-bleed light grey (#F1F1F1) slide with a grey header band across the top and a grey footer band below a white chart panel. The title sits top-left at about x 20% and the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a common left edge. The chart occupies the middle white band, with its title left-aligned above the plot; the source line sits bottom-left and the author, date and slide number sit bottom-right on the same baseline.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled 'US Federal industry-specific regulation index, 2014'. Y-axis: unitless index, linear scale from 0 to 30 in steps of 5, with light dotted horizontal gridlines at each step. X-axis: ten industry categories, left to right: Cargo shipping, Pharma, Oil & Gas, Fishing, Airlines, Banks, Finance, Cars, Electrical utilities, Refining. Values are not printed on the slide; estimated from the axis: Cargo shipping about 11.3, Pharma about 11.5, Oil & Gas about 12, Fishing about 13.2, Airlines about 13.3, Banks about 16, Finance about 16.5, Cars about 16.7, Electrical utilities about 21, Refining about 25.5. Single series, bars ordered ascending by value. Bar colours: mid grey #7F7F7F for Cargo shipping, Pharma, Oil & Gas, Fishing, Airlines, Cars and Refining; lighter grey #909090 for Finance and Electrical utilities; Banks highlighted in red #EB483F. No legend. Bars are about 3.8% of slide width each, with a thin dark grey baseline at 0. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands, white #FFFFFF for the central chart band. Title in Photina (a serif, regular weight) in #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in Photina regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title in Helvetica Neue Bold (sans-serif), #545554, about 2.2% of slide height. Axis and category labels in Helvetica Neue regular, #535353 for y-axis values and #545554 for category labels, about 1.85% of slide height. Source line in Helvetica Neue regular #545454, about 1.85%. Footer author name in Helvetica Neue Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number in Helvetica Neue regular #A6A6A6, about 1.85%. Bars are flat with no outlines, corners or shadows. Gridlines are fine dotted grey lines. No gradients.

### Slide 89

**Text**

We don’t actually regulate ‘banks’, or ‘money’
We won’t regulate ‘tech’ – we’ll have lots of different regulation for different issues and companies
‘Regulate the banks’
Retail deposits
Public securities markets
Insider trading
Credit cards
Pensions
Capital adequacy
Mortgages
Futures & options
And so much more…
Benedict Evans — February 2020
89

**Layout**

Two-column composition below a left-aligned title block. The title and subtitle start at about 20% of slide width and sit near the top (y 5.5% and 13.1%). On the left, a red circle sits at x 20.1–40.8%, y 35.2–72.0%, with a grey arrow to its right at x 47.2%. On the right, a 3×3 grid of cells spans x 57.6–95%, y 20.9–86.6%. The footer (Benedict Evans — February 2020) is right-aligned at y 91.1% and the page number 89 sits at x 94.4% near the right margin. Left margin is about 20%, right margin about 5%.

**Visuals**

Shapes only, no photographs, charts or logos. (1) Solid red circle, fill #EB483F, diameter about 20.7% of slide width, centred at about (30.9%, 53.6%), with the white text ‘Regulate the / banks’ centred inside. (2) Solid right-pointing block arrow, fill #BFBFBF, x 47.2%, y 48.4%, w 4.0%, h 10.4%, with a rectangular shaft about half the arrow height and a triangular head; no outline, no shadow. (3) A 3×3 grid of rectangular cells with about 1.4% gutters, columns starting at x 57.6, 70.0 and 82.5%, rows at y 20.9, 42.6 and 64.3%, each cell 12.5% by 21.7%. Eight cells are solid white #FFFFFF with no outline or shadow. The bottom-right cell (‘And so much more…’) has no white fill and is nearly the same tone as the background. Row 1: Retail deposits / Public securities markets / Insider trading. Row 2: Credit cards / Pensions / Capital adequacy. Row 3: Mortgages / Futures & options / And so much more… A faint lighter panel sits behind the grid as a backing frame.

**Design**

Background flat light grey #F1F1F1. Titles and body text in dark grey #545454; the subtitle is the same grey. Footer text is lighter grey #A6A6A6. Accent is the red circle #EB483F and the grey arrow #BFBFBF. Title and subtitle are set in Photina MT, a transitional serif, regular weight; cell and circle labels use the same serif. Footer uses Helvetica Neue, sans-serif, with the name in bold. Title is about 5.6% of slide height and the subtitle about 2.6%. Cell and circle text is about 3.0% and 3.3%, footer about 1.9%. Shapes have square corners, no borders, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 90

**Text**

Is this a ‘tech’ problem? / Is this a new problem, or an old problem expressed in a new way? / Old issues, / old solutions / Online-only banks / Old issues, / new questions / Uber / New issues, / new solutions / Airbnb, Facebook / Benedict Evans / —— February 2020 / 90

**Layout**

Three equal white circles sit in a single horizontal row across the middle-upper area of the slide, separated by two grey right-pointing arrows, with the row spanning roughly x 19% to 96%. The title sits top-left at x 20.1%, y 5.5%, left-aligned on a shared left edge with the subtitle directly beneath it, leaving the left margin empty below. The footer line 'Benedict Evans — February 2020' and the page number '90' sit at the bottom right, around y 91%, with the page number at the far right edge.

**Visuals**

Three identical white circles, each with a 2-3 pixel red outline (about #E84C3D) and no fill pattern, each containing a bold two-line heading and a regular one-line caption. Two solid grey (#BFBFBF) block arrows point right, one between circle 1 and circle 2 and one between circle 2 and circle 3, each with a rectangular shaft and triangular head, no outline, no shadow. Circle 1 contains 'Old issues, old solutions' and 'Online-only banks'. Circle 2 contains 'Old issues, new questions' and 'Uber'. Circle 3 contains 'New issues, new solutions' and 'Airbnb, Facebook'. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the full slide. Flat light grey background (#F1F1F1) with no gradient or texture. Title and subtitle in dark grey #545454; circle headings in bold grey #545454; circle captions in regular grey #545454; footer and page number in light grey #A6A6A6. Typefaces: PhotinaMT serif (regular and SemiBold) for title, subtitle, circle text; HelveticaNeue sans-serif (bold for the name, regular for the date and number) for footer. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, circle headings about 3.3%, circle captions about 3.0%, footer about 1.9%. Circles have no shadow; arrows are flat grey.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

Approach Rule Capital Planning / Liquidity ST BHC and FBO / FSB Data Gap Initiative / Reg cap CCP exposures / LCR / CCAR /DFAST / CRDIV / G-SIB Surcharge / ST / ECB CA / Vickers Reform / B3 (IRB) / PRA Int'l banks / EBA SREP / EBA Liquidity monitoring / Financial conglomerates / LCR / G-SIB Surcharge / BCBS 239 / FSB Data Gap Initiative / SA-CCR / Reg cap CCP exposures / BoE/PRA ST / EU-wide ST / PRA CAD/ Pillar 2 / FSB Data Gap Initiative / D-SIB framework / Reporting fin. conglomerates / LCR / LCR / CA res. mortgage / B3 ratios / Basel 2 / Basel 3 buffers / FSB Data Gap Initiative / BCBS 239 / Reg cap CCP exposures / G-SIB Surcharge / New securitization framework / FRTB / CVA review / IFRS 9 / Leverage Ratio / IRRBB review / Revised SA approach CR / NSFR / Leverage Ratio / TLAC / Concentration Large Exposures / FRTB / ASIA PACIFIC / AMERICAS / 2019 / 2018 / 2017 / 2016 / 2015 / 2016 / 2017 / 2018 / 2019 / Benedict Evans / –– February 2020 / 93

**Layout**

Left-aligned title and subtitle sit in the top band starting at x 20.1%, with the title on one line at y 5.5% and the subtitle beneath it at y 13.1%. A large semicircular diagram is centred horizontally below them, spanning roughly x 20% to 86% and y 24% to 98%. The footer author and date line sits at y 91.1% on the right, followed by the page number at x 94.4%, with no margins drawn.

**Visuals**

One semicircular nested-arc diagram (a radial timeline of regulatory items) with three outer bands: ASIA PACIFIC on the left in green about #1DB584, EMEA across the top in light blue about #A3C6EA, and AMERICAS on the right in dark blue about #2453A8, with the band names set in white capitals rotated along the arcs. Inside the bands are concentric pale grey arcs about #E3E4E8 separated by white lines, and radial white lines divide the arc into segments. Year labels 2019, 2018, 2017, 2016, 2015, 2016, 2017, 2018 and 2019 sit along the bottom baseline in dark grey, and small rotated year labels 2019, 2018, 2017, 2016, 2016, 2017, 2018 and 2019 mark the arc boundaries in white. Each item is a small blue globe icon (about #1EA7E1) or a national flag icon (UK, EU, US, Australia, Hong Kong, China, Japan, South Korea, Vietnam, Russia, Singapore, Canada, Turkey) followed by a dark grey label, placed by year and region. No values or axes are printed.

**Design**

Background is flat light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient. Title is a serif (PhotinaMT, close to a Photina-style transitional serif) regular weight in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height, with a letter size of roughly 7.8% for capitals. Subtitle is the same serif in regular weight, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Footer text is a sans-serif (Helvetica Neue) in light grey #A6A6A6; the author name is bold and about 1.85% of slide height, the date and page number regular. The diagram uses white 0.3% lines between shapes, no shadows, and square corners.

### Slide 95

**Text**

Where to start? We have a wish list…
Open / platforms
Police / access
Secure, private, / minimal data / collection
Catch state / ‘bad actors’
Trustworthy / information
Empower / competitors
Moderate / ‘bad’ / content
Benedict Evans — February 2020
95

**Layout**

Single slide with the title set in the upper-left area starting at about 20% from the left, left-aligned on one line near the top. Below it, a cluster of seven red circles is arranged in a loose arc: a large central circle with six smaller circles around it (three on the left, three on the right, with Open platforms top right and Police access top left). The footer sits bottom right, with the author name and date on the left of the footer line and the slide number at the far right, all sharing a baseline near 91% of the height.

**Visuals**

Seven filled circles, all flat #EB483F with no outline or shadow, containing white serif labels. Centre circle (largest, about 21% wide by 37% tall, i.e. a circle about 0.37 of slide height in diameter): 'Secure, private, minimal data collection'. Surrounding circles, each about 11% wide by 20% tall: top-left 'Police access'; top-right 'Open platforms'; middle-left 'Catch state ‘bad actors’'; middle-right 'Trustworthy information'; bottom-left 'Empower competitors'; bottom-right 'Moderate ‘bad’ content'. No chart, photo, icon or logo; no legend.

**Design**

Background flat light grey #F1F1F1. Title in a serif face resembling Photina (regular weight), dark grey #545454, approx 5.6% of slide height for the cap-height line. Circle labels in Photina regular, white #FFFFFF, about 3.0% of slide height. Circles are flat #EB483F with no gradient, no shadow, no stroke. Footer in Helvetica Neue sans-serif: author name bold and date regular, light grey #A6A6A6, about 1.9% of slide height; page number in regular weight same colour. Generous margins, no rules or decorative lines.

### Slide 96

**Text**

All of these are trade-offs
Do we want platforms to know and control what happens on their networks, or not? Or both?
Police access
Open platforms
Catch state ‘bad actors’
Secure, private, minimal data collection
Trustworthy information
Empower competitors
Moderate ‘bad’ content
Benedict Evans — February 2020
96

**Layout**

Left-aligned title and subtitle sit in the top band starting at about 20% of slide width, with the subtitle directly below the title. A hub-and-spoke diagram fills the middle: one large central circle with six smaller circles around it, three on the left and three on the right, arranged roughly symmetrically. The footer line and page number sit bottom-right at about 91% of slide height, with the page number at the far right edge of the text block.

**Visuals**

Hub-and-spoke diagram of seven flat grey circles (#7F7F7F) with white serif labels. The large central circle is labelled 'Secure, private, minimal data collection' and sits at about x 34.9%, y 35.7%, w 21.1%, h 37.2%. Six satellite circles of equal size (w 11.1%, h 19.7%) are placed around it: 'Police access' top-left at x 25.7%, y 22.4%; 'Open platforms' top-right at x 54.1%, y 20.4%; 'Catch state ‘bad actors’' middle-left at x 20.1%, y 45.2%; 'Trustworthy information' middle-right at x 59.7%, y 44.5%; 'Empower competitors' bottom-left at x 26.1%, y 66.9%; 'Moderate ‘bad’ content' bottom-right at x 54.1%, y 66.9%. Each satellite is joined to the central circle by a red (#EE4B3F) double-headed arrow with filled triangular heads, approximately 3 to 4% of slide width long, drawn diagonally from the upper-left, upper-right, left, right, lower-left and lower-right edges of the hub. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Plain light grey background (#F1F1F1) with no gradient or texture. Title in a light serif (PhotinaMT-like, regular weight) in dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height, in sentence-case. Subtitle in the same serif, regular weight, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Circle labels in white (#FFFFFF) serif regular, about 3.0% of slide height, centred. Footer in Helvetica Neue: 'Benedict Evans' in bold and the date and page number in regular, all #A6A6A6, about 1.9% of slide height. Circles are flat #7F7F7F with no outline and no shadow. Connector arrows are red #EE4B3F, about 0.5% of slide height thick, with filled triangular heads at both ends; no shadows.

### Slide 97

**Text**

Easy to say, but do we know what we want?
There’s bad stuff on your platform – take it down
Benedict Evans — February 2020

**Visuals**

Three highlight boxes behind words of the central sentence: each is a white rectangle (#FFFFFF) with a solid red-orange (#EB483F) bar along its bottom edge, sitting behind the words 'bad stuff', 'platform' and 'take it down'. No photographs, charts or icons.

**Design**

Background: Flat light grey #F1F1F1. Plain light grey background (#F1F1F1). Title in PhotinaMT serif regular, dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height in capitals-and-lowercase. Subtitle in PhotinaMT serif regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Central sentence in PhotinaMT serif regular, #545454, about 5.2% of slide height. Footer in Helvetica Neue, light grey #A6A6A6, about 1.9% of slide height; author name in Helvetica Neue Bold. Highlight boxes are flat white rectangles with a red-orange bottom bar of about 1.2% of slide height, no corner radius, no shadow, no gradient.

### Slide 98

**Text**

What does ‘take down’ mean?
‘Social networks’ are a mix of many publishing forms with different speech and distribution models
Advertising
Recommendation engines
Public feeds
Groups
Private feeds
Group
messages
1:1
Source: Alex Stamos
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
98

**Layout**

Single-column slide with the title and subtitle left-aligned at about 20% from the left edge, starting near the top margin (title top at 5.5% of height). A centred inverted funnel of seven stacked tiers sits horizontally centred on the slide between about 31.7% and 81.4% of height. Footer line at the bottom: source credit flush left at 20.1% and the author, date and page number grouped at the bottom right, ending near 95.6% of width.

**Visuals**

Diagram: an inverted pyramid (funnel) made of seven stacked trapezoidal tiers, each a flat-bottomed band, widest at the top and narrowing to a small point at the bottom. Tier 1 (top, widest, about 61.3% of slide width, fill #D9D9D9) labelled Advertising. Tiers 2 to 7 (fill #BFBFBF) labelled in order: Recommendation engines, Public feeds, Groups, Private feeds, Group messages, 1:1. Each tier is separated from the next by a thin light gap of about 0.4% of height in the background colour. Tiers step inward by about 4.4% of width per side. Labels are centred white serif text. No arrows, connectors, icons, photos or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide. Flat, minimal look on a light grey background #F1F1F1. Title and subtitle in dark grey #545454 serif (PhotinaMT regular). Title approximately 5.6% of slide height; subtitle about 2.6%. Tier labels white #FFFFFF serif, about 3.0% of height. Tier fills in light grey #D9D9D9 (top) and mid grey #BFBFBF (the rest). Source line in dark grey #545454 sans-serif (Helvetica Neue regular, about 1.9%). Author name in bold sans-serif #A6A6A6, date and page number in regular sans-serif #A6A6A6. No shadows, gradients, corner radii or strokes.

### Slide 99

**Text**

What does ‘bad’ mean?
It’s not just ‘China’ - liberal democracies have widely varying attitudes to free speech
Percentage believing that people should be free to:
Offend minorities
80%
Offend religion
Be sexually explicit
60%
40%
20%
0%
Brazil
Canada
France
Germany
India
Italy
Japan
Spain
UK
USA
Source: Pew Global Attitudes Survey, 2015
Benedict Evans
–– February 2020
99

**Layout**

Two-band slide: a light grey title band across the top (about 0 to 20.5% of height) holds a left-aligned serif title and subtitle starting at x 20.1%, and a white chart band fills the middle (20.5% to 86.1%). A grey footer band below holds the source line at left (x 20.1%) and the author, date and page number right-aligned to about x 95%. The chart sits in the white band, with a legend at the far left (x 4.8%) and the plot area spanning x 17.5% to 95%.

**Visuals**

Chart: grouped vertical bar chart, three series per country. Title above the plot: 'Percentage believing that people should be free to:'. Y axis: percentage, 0% to 80%, gridlines (dotted, light grey) at 20%, 40%, 60%, 80%, with the 0% baseline solid grey. X axis: ten countries in order Brazil, Canada, France, Germany, India, Italy, Japan, Spain, UK, USA. No values are printed; the estimates below are approximate. Series: red #EB483F = Offend minorities; dark grey #80807F = Offend religion; light grey #BFBFBF = Be sexually explicit. About values, Brazil: red 48, grey 43, light 23. Canada: red 52, grey 64, light 37. France: red 51, grey 53, light 41. Germany: red 27, grey 38, light 23. India: red 26, grey 28, light 22. Italy: red 32, grey 29, light 36. Japan: red 14, grey 24, light 10. Spain: red 57, grey 54, light 70. UK: red 54, grey 57, light 31. USA: red 67, grey 77, light 52. Legend: top-left of the chart, three square swatches stacked vertically, red for Offend minorities, dark grey for Offend religion, light grey for Be sexually explicit. No highlighted points, no photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 top and bottom bands, with a white #FFFFFF middle chart band. Flat, minimal, no gradients or shadows. Background is light grey #F1F1F1 in the top and bottom bands with a pure white #FFFFFF chart band between them. Title is dark grey #545454 in a regular high-contrast serif (Photina MT, roman, about 5.6% of slide height, roughly 7.9% cap-equivalent). Subtitle is dark grey #545454 in the same serif at about 2.6% of height. Chart title is bold sans-serif (Helvetica Neue Bold) #545554 at about 2.2%. Legend and axis labels are regular Helvetica Neue at about 1.85%, dark grey #535353 and #545554. Source line is Helvetica Neue regular #545454 at 1.85%. Footer name is Helvetica Neue Bold #A6A6A6, date and page number regular #A6A6A6, all 1.85%. Bars are solid fills with thin white gaps between them and no outlines. Gridlines are 0.5 px dotted grey. No rounded corners.

**Text**

Do we know what we want?
We lack consensus on what’s bad enough to ‘take down’, and on what ‘take down’ even means
Totally public
Innocuous
Rude?
Offensive?
Illegal
Totally private
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
100

**Layout**

Single-column content slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle starting at about 20% from the left edge, a centred two-axis diagram occupying the middle of the slide, and a small footer line at bottom right with the author name, date and slide number. The title sits at the top, with the subtitle directly beneath it; the axis labels are aligned to the axis lines.

**Visuals**

A two-axis diagram with no chart values. A horizontal dotted double-headed arrow runs across the slide at about 55% height, from about x 23% to x 94%, with labels Innocuous at the left end, Rude? left of centre, Offensive? right of centre and Illegal at the right end. A vertical dotted double-headed arrow crosses it at about x 57%, from about y 27% to y 84%, with the label Totally public at the top and Totally private at the bottom. The arrows are red (about #E84C4C) with filled triangular arrowheads at both ends and fine dotted strokes. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Title in a PhotinaMT-style serif, regular weight, dark grey #545454, about 5.6% of slide height for the capitals-and-lowercase title. Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Axis labels in the same serif, regular, #535353, about 3.0% of slide height. Arrows are red #E84C4C with dotted strokes and filled triangular heads, no shadows or corner radii. Footer in Helvetica Neue, about 1.85% of slide height, colour #A6A6A6: the name in bold and the rest regular.

### Slide 101

**Text**

China, digital sovereignty / and digital extra- / territoriality / Benedict Evans — February 2020 / 101

**Layout**

Single-column title slide on a light grey field. A large pale-red panel sits to the right of a left margin (about 17% from left, ending near the right edge at about 96%), spanning roughly 20% to 81% of the height. The title is left-aligned inside the panel, starting at about 20% from left, and sits in the upper half of the panel. A thin red horizontal rule runs along the bottom edge of the panel. The footer at about 91% height contains the author name in bold followed by a dash and date, with the slide number at the right margin.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background light grey #F1F1F1. Panel fill is a very faint red tint (#EB483F at about 4% opacity, resulting in roughly #EFE9E8), with no shadow, no rounded corners. Title text in red #EB483F, set in a high-contrast serif (Photina-style, regular weight), about 9.3% of slide height per line with line spacing about 11.3% of height. Footer text in grey #A6A6A6 in Helvetica Neue: author name bold, date and slide number regular, about 1.85% of slide height. Red rule #EB483F, roughly 0.3% of height thick, spanning the panel width. No gradients, no shadows.

none

103

**Layout**

Three horizontal bands: a light grey header band across the top (0 to 20.5% of height) holding the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1%; a white content band (20.5 to 86.1%) holding one chart; a light grey footer band (86.1 to 100%) holding the source line at left and the author, date and slide number at right. Left text margin is 20.1% of width; the slide number sits at x 93.8% and the chart's right edge reaches about 95.3%.

**Visuals**

One stacked column chart, titled 'Smartphone users, June 2019 (bn)', with a vertical axis labelled with values 0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 2.5, 3.0 (units: billions of users, range 0.0 to 3.0, gridlines every 0.5, dotted light grey lines) and two categories on the horizontal axis: 'China & India' and 'RoW'. Column 'China & India' (about 1.35 bn total, no value printed): bottom segment 'China' about 0.9 bn in red #EB483F; top segment 'India' about 0.45 bn in pale pink #FBDAD9; both labels are printed inside the segments. Column 'RoW' (about 2.65 bn total, no value printed): bottom segment 'North America' about 0.27 bn in dark grey #7F7F7F; next segment 'Western Europe' about 0.35 bn in mid grey #A6A6A6; top segment 'Other' about 2.0 bn in light grey #BFBFBF. Segments are separated by thin white hairlines. No legend; the segment names serve as labels. No points are highlighted. Baseline at 0.0 is a solid thin grey line. No photographs, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Flat light grey #F1F1F1 covering the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF band from 20.5% to 86.1% of height. Flat, minimal, no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Background is light grey #F1F1F1 for the header and footer bands with a white #FFFFFF content band. Title in PhotinaMT serif regular, colour #545454, approximately 5.6% of slide height (cap height about 3.9%). Subtitle in PhotinaMT serif regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title and all axis and category labels in Helvetica Neue, #545454 (chart title in Helvetica Neue Bold), about 1.85% of slide height. Footer text in Helvetica Neue #A6A6A6 for the author, date and slide number, with the author name in bold; source line in Helvetica Neue #545454. Accent colours are the red #EB483F and the pinks and greys of the bars. Segment labels are white text except the 'India' label which is #535353. Bars are flat fills with thin white separators.

**Layout**

Full-width light grey header band holds the title and subtitle left-aligned at about 20% from the left edge, with a white body area holding a single stacked column chart, and a light grey footer band with source line at left and author, date and page number at right. The title sits at the top of the header band, the subtitle directly beneath it, and the page number sits at the far right of the footer at about 94% of the width.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart titled 'Ecommerce, 2019 ($tr)' with a y-axis labelled 0.0 to 2.0 in steps of 0.5 (units $tr, shown in the chart title) and dotted horizontal gridlines at each labelled value. Two columns: 'China & India' and 'Other'. The 'China & India' column stacks China (red, about 1.5, the height reads to the 1.5 gridline) and a thin pale pink India segment on top (about 0.04, reaching about 1.54), labelled 'India' above the column. The 'Other' column stacks North America (dark grey #7F7F7F, about 0.0 to 0.66), Western Europe (mid grey #A6A6A6, about 0.66 to 1.0) and Other (light grey #BFBFBF, about 1.0 to 1.95, top about 1.96), each labelled inside the segment in white. No legend; segment labels serve as legend. Values are not printed; the values above are estimates. No highlighted points. Source line: McKinsey, eMarketer.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 header and footer bands framing a white #FFFFFF body band. Light grey background #F1F1F1 for header and footer bands with a white #FFFFFF body band. Title in a serif face resembling Photina, regular weight, dark grey #545454, large (about 5.6% of slide height). Subtitle in the same serif, regular, #545454, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart title in a bold sans-serif resembling Helvetica Neue Bold, #545554, about 2.2%. Axis and segment labels in Helvetica Neue regular, about 1.85%, #545554 for axes, white for in-segment labels. Source line in Helvetica Neue regular #545454; author name in bold #A6A6A6 and date and page number in regular #A6A6A6, all about 1.85%. Bars have square corners, no outlines, with thin white separators between stacked segments. Gridlines are thin dotted grey lines. Accent colour is red #EB483F for China, the only highlighted segment; India is pale pink #F9D3D1.

### Slide 106

**Text**

American companies, global reach / “But the US constitution says!” is no longer an adequate answer / Global user base, Q3 2019 / Rest of World / USA / 100% / 75% / 50% / 25% / 0% / Google / Apple / Facebook / Netflix / Source: Companies, @BenedictEvans / Benedict Evans / — February 2020 / 106

**Layout**

A full-width light grey header band holds the serif title and subtitle, starting at about x 20% and sitting in the upper part of the slide. A white chart panel fills the middle band, with a stacked column chart spanning roughly x 17% to 96%. Legend sits at the far left at about x 3% to 12%, above the chart's left edge. A light grey footer band carries the source line at the left (x 20%) and the author, date and page number at the right, with the page number at about x 94%.

**Visuals**

Chart: 100% stacked column chart, titled 'Global user base, Q3 2019'. Y axis: 0% to 100%, labelled at 0%, 25%, 50%, 75%, 100%, with light grey dotted horizontal gridlines at each label. X axis: four categories, Google, Apple, Facebook, Netflix, with a thin grey baseline. Each column is a stacked pair: USA in grey at the bottom and Rest of World in red at the top. Values (estimated, no values printed): Google USA about 10%, Rest of World about 90%; Apple USA about 17%, Rest of World about 83%; Facebook USA about 12%, Rest of World about 88%; Netflix USA about 42%, Rest of World about 58%. Legend: red square labelled 'Rest of World' above grey square labelled 'USA'. No highlighted points. Columns are flat-filled with thin white separators between segments.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F1F1F1 across the whole slide, with a white #FFFFFF band behind the chart. Light grey #F1F1F1 background for header and footer bands, with a white #FFFFFF chart panel between them. Title in regular serif (Photina-like, closest common serif), colour #545454, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in the same serif, #545454, about 2.6%. Chart title in Helvetica Neue Bold, #545454, about 2.2%. Axis labels, legend and category labels in Helvetica Neue regular, #545454, about 1.85%. Footer text in Helvetica Neue, #A6A6A6, about 1.85%, with 'Benedict Evans' in bold. Accent colours: red #EB483F for Rest of World, grey #7F7F7F for USA. Flat shapes with square corners, no shadows or gradients. Gridlines are thin dotted light grey.

### Slide 108

**Text**

Digital extraterritoriality
How do you apply domestic laws to a global medium, and to citizens who may be abroad?
Austria
EU court holds that
an Austrian libel
take-down order
applies globally
Singapore
State’s right-of-
reply must be
applied to posts
seen in Australia
GDPR & CCPA
Laws apply to EU/
CA citizens
wherever they are
Benedict Evans — February 2020 108

**Layout**

Single row of three equal white circles centred horizontally across the lower-middle of the slide, evenly spaced with equal gaps. Title sits top-left at about 20% from the left edge, with a one-line subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on the same left edge. Footer with author name, date and slide number sits bottom-right, on one baseline near 91% of slide height; no visible logo, no grid lines.

**Visuals**

Three identical white circles with thin red outlines, each containing a centred heading and a short centred body text. Circle 1 (left): heading 'Austria', body 'EU court holds that / an Austrian libel / take-down order / applies globally'. Circle 2 (middle): heading 'Singapore', body 'State’s right-of-reply must be applied to posts seen in Australia'. Circle 3 (right): heading 'GDPR & CCPA', body 'Laws apply to EU/CA citizens wherever they are'. No photographs, charts, icons or logos. Circles are flat fills with no shadow, no gradient, no icons.

**Design**

Background flat light grey #F1F1F1. Titles and headings in dark grey #545454 serif (PhotinaMT, regular weight). Subtitle and body text in the same dark grey #545454 serif (PhotinaMT, regular). Footer author name in bold grey #A6A6A6 sans-serif (HelveticaNeue-Bold), footer date and number in regular grey #A6A6A6 sans-serif (HelveticaNeue). Title about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, circle headings about 3.3%, body about 3.0%, footer about 1.9%. Circles: white #FFFFFF fill, red #E84C3D outline about 0.3% of slide width, perfectly round corners, no shadow, no gradient.

### Slide 109

**Text**

Global reach means a lowest common denominator
Extra-territorial regulation means companies start complying with the harshest rule that applies, wherever it is
Chicago Tribune
[...]ely, our website is currently unavailable in most
[...]n countries. We are engaged on the issue and
committed to looking at options that support our full range of
digital offerings to the EU market. We continue to identify
technical compliance solutions that will provide all readers
with our award-winning journalism.
Los Angeles Times
Unfortunately, our website is currently unavailable in most
European countries. We are engaged on the issue and committed
to looking at options that support our full range of digital
offerings to the EU market. We continue to identify technical
compliance solutions that will provide all readers with our award-
winning journalism.
Benedict Evans
— February 2020
109

**Layout**

Single slide with a left-aligned title and subtitle across the top, starting at about 20% from the left edge. Two web-page screenshots sit in the centre-right and overlap diagonally: the Chicago Tribune box is lower and further right, and the Los Angeles Times box is higher and to the left, covering the left part of the Chicago box. Footer line with author name, date and page number sits at the bottom right, around 91% from the top.

**Visuals**

Two boxed screenshots of newspaper web pages, both showing the same notice. Left, on top: Los Angeles Times box with a light grey masthead band containing the black blackletter-style wordmark 'Los Angeles Times' centred, and a white body with centred black serif text of six lines. Right, behind: Chicago Tribune box with a dark blue masthead band (about #00508A) containing the white blackletter wordmark 'Chicago Tribune' centred, and a white body with dark grey serif text of five visible lines; the left part of its text and masthead is hidden under the Los Angeles Times box. Both boxes have a thin mid-grey (about #999999) 1px border, square corners, no shadow, no gradient, flat, not cropped. No photographs, icons or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat light grey #F1F1F1 fill covering the entire slide. Flat light grey background #F1F1F1 with no gradient or texture. Title and subtitle in PhotinaMT-style serif (Times-like, regular weight) in dark grey #545454. Footer author name in bold Helvetica Neue-style sans-serif #A6A6A6, date and page number in regular weight #A6A6A6. Title about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 2.6%, footer about 1.9%. Screenshot body text is a Times-like serif, black on the Los Angeles Times box and dark grey on the Chicago Tribune box. Accent colour is the Chicago Tribune blue #00508A. Lines are thin grey borders, no rounded corners, no shadows.

### Slide 110

**Text**

Tiktok: not just your standards? / When the community standards being enforced / may not be your community’s standards / Benedict Evans / –– January 2020 / –– February 2020 / 110

**Layout**

Two-column split. The left half is a light grey panel holding a left-aligned title and a two-line body text block, vertically centred around the middle of the slide with generous left margin (about 4.6% of width). The right half is a full-height photograph bleeding to the top, bottom and right edges. The footer row sits at about 91% of slide height: 'Benedict Evans' and the date at x 74.9% to 90.8%, and the page number '110' at x 93.8%, all sitting over the photograph area.

**Visuals**

One photograph, occupying the right half of the slide full-bleed (top, bottom and right edges). Subject: a young woman with shoulder-length straight black hair and a centre parting, wearing a black knit crew-neck sweater and a pale blue scrunchie on her left wrist. She holds a silver metal eyelash curler up to her right eye (viewer's left), with her hand raised near her face, and her lips are parted as if speaking. Action: a selfie-style, handheld framing of someone applying makeup at home. Composition: tight crop from roughly the head to the upper torso, face at upper centre-right, hand and curler at left of the face. Lighting: soft, warm, indoor artificial light on the skin. Dominant colours: warm beige skin tones, black sweater, mauve-pink wall at top, and a dusty mauve curtain with silver metallic triangular pattern and grommets at the right. The image appears slightly soft and low resolution, with mild compression artefacts. No overlay or tint is visible. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey #F2F2F2 on the left half, with the photograph covering the right half.

China’s global content moderation
‘Don’t forget Taiwan is part of China’
SWAROVSKI
VALENTINO
McDONALD’S
GIVENCHY
NBA
VERSACE

COACH
Calvin Klein
fresh

Benedict Evans — February 2020
111

**Layout**

A full-width header band sits on a light grey field, with the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 20.1% and a white content band from y 20.5% to 86.1% holding a three-row grid of eleven brand logos, staggered and centred in loose columns. The footer sits at the bottom right, with the author and date at x 74.9% and the page number at x 93.8%, both at y 91.1%.

**Visuals**

Eleven brand logos in black, grey, gold and navy, shown on a white field. Swarovski: a black swan above the bold serif wordmark SWAROVSKI, with the swan's tail fading into a dot pattern. Valentino: the wordmark VALENTINO in a high-contrast serif with tall thin strokes. McDonald’s: the golden double-arch M with a small registered mark at its lower right. Givenchy: a dark grey interlocking square-pattern monogram above the wordmark GIVENCHY in widely spaced sans-serif capitals. NBA: the blue and red NBA logoman silhouette of a dribbling player beside the bold blue block letters NBA. Versace: the silver-grey Medusa head emblem in a circle above the wordmark VERSACE in widely spaced serif capitals. Audi: four interlocking chrome-grey rings in a row. Coach: the wordmark COACH in dark brown serif capitals inside a dark brown outlined rounded-corner badge. Calvin Klein: the wordmark Calvin Klein in thin black geometric sans-serif. fresh: the lowercase italic wordmark fresh in dark slate grey with a small registered mark. GAP: the wordmark GAP in tall narrow black serif capitals with wide letter spacing. No photographs, charts or other graphics.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #F1F1F1 full-slide fill with a white #FFFFFF band from y 20.5% to 86.1%. Light grey background (#F1F1F1) framing the slide, with a pure white band (#FFFFFF) across the middle. The title is a light serif (Photina) in dark grey #545454 at about 5.6% of slide height, and the subtitle is the same family in #545454 at about 2.6%. Footer text is sans-serif (Helvetica Neue), with the name in bold and the rest regular, in light grey #A6A6A6 at about 1.9%. No accent colours beyond the logos themselves; no lines, shadows, gradients or rounded shapes apart from the logos.
